# Decisions

One line each, newest at the bottom. Spec = "Capeline build spec".

- 2026-11-13 (nate, in the spec): 22 weeks with no cuts; PvP toggle at level 10 plus the Comet Crater forced zone; accept a Moderate rating; no typed hero names (title and noun wheels); level cap 40.
- 2026-11-16 (nate): start Capeline now; other new games paused. Build milestone after milestone without pausing for Studio checks.
- 2026-11-16 (nate): Capeline is superhero AND anime. Added at launch: (1) a per-hero Comic or Anime effect style plus anime creator pieces (hair, coats, scarves, wraps, glowing eyes), looks only; (2) Awakening, a meter-filled 15 s power-up for every hero (aura, glow, stronger super); (3) a fifth power set, Blade (energy sword dashes, combo strings, a slash-wave super). About +3 weeks: Phase 1 about 25 weeks. All original, nothing copied from real shows.
- 2026-11-16: the spec's "reuse Hive Hold enemy AI and boss framework" can't happen (Hive Hold isn't built); enemies and bosses are written fresh (EnemySim) in Mossbell Stand's plain-data style.
- 2026-11-16: added an Enemy.Delta remote (10 Hz, unreliable, 10 bytes per enemy, only enemies within 450 studs). The spec's remote list had no enemy replication line.
- 2026-11-16: out-of-combat regen 4% of max health per second after 6 s without damage (Config.Stats.regen). The spec has no PvE healing outside abilities, which would leave solo heroes slowly bleeding out between incidents. (tune)
- 2026-11-16: leaps use their own gravity (airGravity 120) so the spec's apex 40 and range 80 give a floaty 1.6 s arc; Roblox's 196 felt like a hop.
- 2026-11-16: speed check uses a travel budget (cap x 1.15 per second, banking 0.5 s) instead of judging each 0.1 s sample alone, so network bursts never trip it. A fail only pulls the hero back; only teleport-sized jumps count as strikes (3 in 10 minutes kicks, no bans).
- 2026-11-16: Zap Bolt and the other basics keep a 0.5 s fire interval (2 per second) though the spec lists cooldown 0; "0" means no skill cooldown.
- 2026-11-16: M1 slice unlocks Flight for everyone (Config.Dev.unlockAllStyles) so the move-and-fight test needs no levels. Turned off in M3 when levels save (spec: Flight at level 3).
- 2026-11-16: M1 default hero is a level 1 Blaster with Volt (basic + Chain Lightning + Dash Strike) until the creator (M9).
- 2026-11-16: world origin is the map centre: map -2,400 to 2,400, hub -800 to 800, Downtown x -750 to -50, z -350 to 350. The Downtown greybox has a fixed seed (the hub is handcrafted in the spec and never changes with the weekly seed).
- 2026-11-16 (nate): enemies are humanoid robots ("robots that look like humans"): metal android bodies with glowing eyes, walking and punching. Spark Drone becomes a jet-boot flyer, Turret a planted heavy sentry android. Still machines only: defeats are sparks, bolts and parts, never blood.
- 2026-11-16: a hero's skills may come from any unlocked set (spec: "Any class can use any set"; the 52-point cap only bites when mixing sets). The primary set (hero.setId) gives the basic attack and the look.
- 2026-11-16: Blade set (abilities 33-40) numbers: Blade Slash 0.65/0.65/1.0 combo, Flash Step 1.4 dash 35, Crescent Wave 1.3 line 50, Rising Blade 1.5 knockup, Thousand Cuts 8 x 0.35 flurry, Parry Stance 1 s counter 2.0, Blade Storm 0.35 per tick r10 4 s, Heaven Splitter super 4.2 line 80 x 14. Unlocks at level 18. (tune)
- 2026-11-16: Awakening numbers: meter 100; +1.5 per basic hit, +4 per skill hit, +8 per super hit, +6 per kill, +0.5 per 1% health lost; 15 s of +20% damage, skill cooldowns 35% faster, supers +30% coeff and half cooldown. Same for every hero; nothing sold touches it. (tune)
- 2026-11-16: supers have 3 ranks: 2 = +10% coeff and the mod pick, 3 = capstone and -8% cooldown. Rank Credits cost is waived (Config.Dev.freeRankCredits) until Credits exist in M3.
- 2026-11-16: Rally Roar gives +10% Power, not "+10% Might" (Might is 0 without gear). Bruiser hits taunt the target 2 s. Loadouts change anywhere out of combat (5 s since damage) until safehouses exist in M4 (superseded by the M4 safehouse rule below).
- 2026-11-16: 4 more team-up combos for the Blade set (Storm Edge, Ice Bloom, Sky Breaker, Void Cut), 10 in all.
- 2026-11-16: melee basics (Punch Combo, Blade Slash) swing every 0.5 s like the ranged basics; their combo strings average about 1.4x a ranged basic's damage for the risk of being close. A level 10 Burglar Bot falls in about 2.1 to 2.3 s to any basic (spec test: 2 to 3 s).
- 2026-11-16: Enemy.Delta grew to 12 bytes per enemy (status flags and facing for the robot bodies); 60 enemies still fit in under 900 bytes.
- 2026-11-16: M3 gear. A Common piece (no bonus stats) puts its whole budget in the main stat; the spec's 70% main share applies when there are bonus stats to take the rest.
- 2026-11-16: upgrade step cost follows the spec formula round(12 x n^1.5 x tier mult): +10 on a Common is 379 (the spec text says 380).
- 2026-11-16: supers rank for 300, 900 and 2,400 Credits (spec Sinks table), not the skill prices.
- 2026-11-16: pity counts the boss in hand: the 5th boss in a row with no Epic is an Epic, the 15th MVP roll in a row with no Legendary is a Legendary (spec test wording). Fixed story rewards (the Act 1 finale Epic) skip the level 20 Epic gate; every other Epic or Legendary above the hero's gate drops one tier lower.
- 2026-11-16: the 5th power set (Blade) gets a 5th Epic set, Edgewalker: 2 pieces Blade damage +8%, 4 pieces Parry Stance ripostes heal 5%. Ironclad's shockwave is 0.3 coeff per 0.5 s for 3 s; Orbitwear's Float heal reaches allies within 12 studs of the lifted target (Float lifts machines).
- 2026-11-16: a respec refunds the Credits its ranks cost into the bank, so trying builds stays free (spec: respec is free).
- 2026-11-16: gadget actives (Grappling Hook 30 s, Smoke Bomb 40 s, Shield Drone 45 s, Decoy 60 s) ship in M3 with the gadget slot; about 6% of drops are gadgets.
- 2026-11-16: server close waits up to 25 s for saves (Roblox allows 30); the spec's 4 s deadline would only drop slow saves. Saves also go 8 s after a level up, drop, gear change, rank or loadout change (one write per burst).
- 2026-11-16: a full stash (150) turns a new drop into Lumen Dust; its style still unlocks.
- 2026-11-16: Profile.Sync sends the full view after gear changes and a small wallet-only view after rewards and spending.
- 2026-11-16: until incidents and bosses exist (M6), nothing in play drops gear or pays Renown; the Dev panel rolls any source and adds Renown, Credits and Dust.
- 2026-11-16: M4 tile hash is FNV-1a (32-bit) over the 4,608-byte tile array, not SHA: Luau has no SHA built in, and the hash only has to match between two servers. Logged as "[CityBuilder] seed N tile hash X" and set as Workspace attributes WorldSeed and WorldHash.
- 2026-11-16: zone rings count tiles out from the hub edge: suburbs 1 to 7 (the edge wobbles by one tile in 4 x 4 blocks so the ring is not a square), farmland 8 to 14, coast 15 and 16. The spec's "suburbs 8 to 14 tiles from the hub edge" would leave rings 1 to 7 empty, so suburbs take the inner half and farmland the outer.
- 2026-11-16: each tile is 2 bytes: zone, variant and rotation as the spec says, plus 4 road bits (local and highway, each direction). Roads run through tile centres (highway every 6 tiles, 30 wide; local every 3, 16 wide) as long strips; each tile splits into 4 lots around its roads and a variant names what stands on each lot (suburbs 12, farmland 10, coast 4, hub filler 6). Rotation turns the lot pattern.
- 2026-11-16: the hub is code-built from fixed seeds (Downtown, the Docks, Old Town, filler tiles between them), so it never changes with the weekly seed. The spec's saved handcrafted hub model can replace it at the M5 kit pass.
- 2026-11-16: 16 safehouses: Downtown 4 on reserved lots (the rest of Downtown is unchanged), Docks 3, Old Town 3, 2 hub parks, 4 outskirts (Northgate, Eastfield, Southmoor, Westbrook) on tiles kept off the road lines. Beacons: Beacon Tower, Downtown East roof, Harbor Crane, Clock Tower, the 3 landmarks, the 4 outskirts safehouse roofs and the Windmill.
- 2026-11-16: the bank, loadout and respawn terminals are ProximityPrompts (key B, gamepad X). The bank is a 3 s hold that moves every on-hand Credit into the bank; the prompt itself is the server check, so there is no Bank.Deposit remote.
- 2026-11-16: loadout changes and respec now need a safehouse (40 studs) or HQ (80 studs of Beacon Tower or the Foundry), or the Quick Swap pass for loadouts; this replaces the M2 "anywhere out of combat" rule. Rank-ups and mods still work anywhere.
- 2026-11-16: respawns: a defeat lands at the nearest visited safehouse (Plaza West is everyone's home); a returning player joins at their chosen safehouse; a new hero lands on the plaza until the M7 opening. Walking onto a safehouse marks it visited and makes it the respawn point if none is chosen yet; the Respawn terminal chooses one.
- 2026-11-16: fast travel: the server checks the beacon, combat and the 20 s cooldown (from departure), the client plays a 3 s camera flight with SKIP, and the server moves the hero when it ends; the hero is untargetable on the way and the destination is streamed in first.
- 2026-11-16: streaming: Roblox has one streaming radius per place (600, the medium preset), lowered by the engine on low-memory phones; the presets' radius numbers stay as targets for the M10 pass. StreamOutBehavior is Opportunistic; hub chunks use the StreamingMesh level of detail so the skyline shows far away; each outskirts tile is one Atomic model.
- 2026-11-16: the world is about 36,900 parts (hub 2,600 greybox, outskirts and landmarks 34,100, base 150): about 16.6 per outskirts tile against the 25 budget. The whole world as data builds in 0.06 s in Lune; parts build 40 tiles a frame after the hub.
- 2026-11-16: the ocean runs 2,000 studs past the map edge at y -2 and can be stood on (PLACEHOLDER); a soft push back at the map edge comes with the M8 anti-exploit pass.
- 2026-11-16: profile version 2 adds per-character beacons and safehouses bit masks; a version 1 save keeps its last safehouse as visited.
- 2026-11-16: M5 the 220 wreckable hub buildings are every Downtown building (92), the 6 Docks warehouses and 122 Old Town houses picked by a fixed hash; outskirts houses, the Foundry, cranes and the Clock Tower crack but never fall. HP by height: up to 30 studs small (6,000), up to 150 medium (10,000), taller towers 14,000.
- 2026-11-16: M5 the server keeps only BuildingState (HP and 2-bit state per building); clients split each building with the shared, seeded Chunker (10 to 40 chunks in floor rows) and draw Damaged, collapse and rubble themselves, so every device drops the same chunks.
- 2026-11-16: M5 "at most 3 collapses at once" means 3 collapses started within the 6 s collapse animation; a 4th is cut to Damaged at 1 HP.
- 2026-11-16: M5 the snapshot is sent when the client asks (BuildingSync, once its listeners are up) so a joining player never misses a diff; diffs batch at 2 Hz with drone jobs. BuildingImpact (center, radius, crater or cracks) goes to players within 300 studs, at most 6 a second per server.
- 2026-11-16: M5 structure damage: an ability's structDmg, or 3,000 for a super without one, where the ability lands (bolts, chains, dashes, cones, arcs, novas, lines; leaps and throws on landing). Buffs, walls, ramps and fields never damage buildings.
- 2026-11-16: M5 a heavy hit is a hit of 2.5x Power or more (Combat.heavyCoeff): it flips vehicles within 10 studs of the target and, if the robot is within 20 studs of a wall facing away from the hero, knocks it 26 studs at the wall and the wall turns Damaged (3 per player per 10 s).
- 2026-11-16: M5 collapsed buildings stop colliding and block no shots on the server until repaired; enemy heights drop to the rubble heap (12 studs at most).
- 2026-11-16: M5 40 hub vehicles are anchored part models placed from fixed street slots; a flip sets the landed pose on the server at once and clients play the 1 s arc from the shared VehiclePose maths, so no physics is replicated.
- 2026-11-16: M5 citizens are server data like the robots (CitizenSim on the sidewalk corner graph, 10 Hz) sent to each player within 250 studs at 4 Hz in a 9-byte-a-citizen unreliable Citizen.Delta (not in the spec's remote list). Danger comes from the 75% warning, collapses and every structural hit; shelters are the Downtown and Old Town safehouses and the Beacon Tower door.
- 2026-11-16: M5 one shared client debris budget (DebrisBudget, the preset's debris count) covers robot defeat bolts, falling chunks and vehicle sparks; dust clouds are limited by the preset's emitter count.
- 2026-11-16: M6 zone level is the hero's own level raised to the area floor (hub 1, suburbs 5, farmland 12, coast 20, Comet Crater 10, Old Quarry 20, cap 40); events and the boss use the median level of the heroes in the server, raised the same way.
- 2026-11-16: M6 rescues and the Contract Board use ProximityPrompts (hold B / gamepad X) as the server's check, so Rescue.Pick and Rescue.Drop have no remotes; carrying a citizen into a drop circle is the drop. Citizens can never be hurt: a defeated carrier sets them down where they were.
- 2026-11-16: M6 the M1 ambient robot spawner is off (EnemyService.populate false): incidents, contracts, events and the boss bring every robot. DevPanel's Spawner button turns it back on for load tests.
- 2026-11-16: M6 the spec has no random event reward row: each participant gets 60 + 4 x L Credits and 80 + 6 x L Renown (times the friend and Legacy bonus) plus one "event" gear roll; in the Meteor shower the roll is the shard itself. Participants are everyone who hit its robots, carried, held, hit the train or grabbed a shard, plus anyone within 150 studs when it clears. A failed event pays nothing.
- 2026-11-16: M6 events happen where they make sense: Meteor shower at the plaza, Tidal surge at the Docks, Blackout in Old Town, Train hijack on a Downtown tram line, Robot rampage and Vault breach near a random player. Never in a mission pocket (y 2,000 and up).
- 2026-11-16: M6 boss cosmetics: Scrapjaw Visor (head, Rustjaw Prime) and Maw Shard Fins (back, Lumen Maw), each with a shiny version at 1 in 20 (tier "Boss" styles). Style chance by tier: MVP 100%, Contributor 50%, Participant 20%. The Rogues File page goes to every tier on a defeat.
- 2026-11-16: M6 tiers: MVP top 3, Contributor top 50%, Participant at 10% of the median score or more; anyone below that who scored at all gets the 25% floor (late joiners). A retreat pays 25% of the tier's Credits only.
- 2026-11-16: M6 support score: shield points and healing given to allies count 1 for 1 (HeroService.Supported), a rescue during the fight counts 400 and a revive 800 (M8); breaking a cannon or generator adds 1,000 on top of the damage done to it.
- 2026-11-16: M6 Raid prep: each Raid prep incident cleared in the last 5 minutes before a boss takes 3% off its HP, at most 15%.
- 2026-11-16: M6 schedule: a fresh server's first boss lands after 10 minutes; a player with stats.bossesJoined = 0 pulls the next boss to at most 6 minutes after they load (the "within 8 minutes" check). bossesJoined counts once per fight in BossService, not per drop.
- 2026-11-16: M6 arenas: Rustjaw Prime lands on the street just south of the plaza and walks toward the crowd within 220 studs of it; the Lumen Maw rises in the Old Quarry and only turns. Both bosses are client-drawn (WorldFx) from Boss.State; the server only has hit targets (EnemyService.addTarget).
- 2026-11-16: M6 the Contract Board is a 4th terminal at every safehouse. Contracts open at level 4, max 3 accepted, one of each type. A saved contract keeps its seed and the board it came from, so a rejoin rebuilds the same sites. The escort truck follows a 16-leg street path; its progress is scaled to the spec's 600 studs. A Stakeout Sneak Bot beaten before its photo is replaced nearby.
- 2026-11-16: M6 remotes beyond the spec's list: ContractBoard (the board screen) and BossAttack (telegraphs and strikes for the effects). Incident.Update is one batched array per player at 2 Hz.
- 2026-11-16: M6 the day and night is client-side lighting: the server sends only the cycle start (World.Clock on join and every 60 s, plus the Workspace attribute); night gets a cool ambient so the city stays readable. Villain incidents and contracts are 30% likelier at night; nothing is locked to night.
- 2026-11-16: tooling: luau-lsp 1.32.0 (MIT) now downloads fine; `luau-lsp analyze` with the 1.32.0 Roblox definitions runs as an extra check (its nonstrict warnings about optional values are noise; read new errors in changed files).
- 2026-11-16: M7 the opening runs per player on the server (OpeningService) on a route OpeningPlan picks from the world: a 30 to 90 stud roof near the plaza by a tram line, the next roof within one Leap, the hanging tram car below, the shard on open ground, a rooftop pad 120 to 200 away (Save Her) and a Docks-side point 260 to 340 away (Grab It). The tram car, shard and beams are drawn on that player's device only; the drones and the citizen are real.
- 2026-11-16: M7 "only verb buttons": during the opening the HUD shows just the step's button (LEAP, GLIDE, CATCH, ZAP) pulsing; every other screen and number is hidden, the sky holds at dusk (18.2), and the only text pops are the +50 Renown catch and the choice buttons. The mentor voice is a sound (Launch Checklist). A stuck opening goes to Suit Up after 4 minutes.
- 2026-11-16: M7 the opening's drones use story tuning on EnemySim: hitLife (the first falls to any 2 hits, the second to 4) and dmgMult (0 never fires, 0.35 hits weakly), so "2 hits of health" holds for every class and set.
- 2026-11-16: M7 Suit Up is a class pick and Hero or Villain (preset by Save Her or Grab It) until the M9 creator. It grants mission 1's first clear: Renown up to level 3 (Flight unlocks there), +1 Skill Point, then a skippable guided-flight marker to the beacon nearest the plaza. Characters already past level 1 when save version 3 arrived skip the opening.
- 2026-11-16: M7 missions are entered from the Journal anywhere out of combat (the spec's HQ replay board waits for the M9 HQ) and are solo until squads (M10); Mission.Enter carries a reserved party id. Minimum levels: 3, 4, 5, 6, 8. The hero fades into a free pocket at its spawn and returns to where they left. 7 pockets means at most 7 runs at once per server; an 8th is told to try again in a minute.
- 2026-11-16: M7 pockets are 400 x 400 at y 3,000 in a row at z 1,900, built on demand from PocketLayout and destroyed after; glass walls seal them. Robots inside stand on the pocket's own boxes (EnemySim heightAt takes the asker's y; EnemyService.setPocketHeight). No incidents, events, world boss packets or ambient robots reach a hero in a scene (hero.scene).
- 2026-11-16: M7 mission steps: hold (3 rings, 10 s each, while waves of drones and burglars come), break (14-hit shard crates with 2 sentries each; the 30-hit runaway train), chase (a Sneak Bot hopping the pocket's roofs), reach (the tower top; the gauntlet end past 4 sentries, 2 with Trust Her), boss (Null Sentinel x8 or x6 HP with adds), defeat (a room squad), choice and warframe. Hits on crates and the train count 1 each whatever the damage.
- 2026-11-16: M7 every clear pays the Economy "mission" row; only the first adds the mission's loot (Rare for 2, 3 and 5; Rare plus Epic for 6), Signal Tower's 600 Renown, the mission Skill Point (1 and 6), 50 own-side reputation (+120 for The Informant) and Test Her's 10% Epic crate at the end of The Informant. A replay keeps the first choice and says so.
- 2026-11-16: M7 the War-Frame: 36,000 HP at level 20 scaled like robots by the hero's level (party median in M10); the 2 shoulder cannons in phase 1; at 66% it summons 6 Spark Drones and raises a shield only 3 hits on the back core break; at 33% the floor fire and a charged laser that the 6 pillars block. It never retreats; a defeated hero respawns at the pocket spawn and the frame keeps its HP, so a wipe is a retry.
- 2026-11-16: M7 choice effects: Save the Train +100 City rep and a Rare Decoy gadget piece; Chase the Thief +100 own-side rep and 600 Credits; Trust Her 2 sentries in the gauntlet; Test Her an ambush squad in The Informant and the 10% Epic crate; Spare the Core the Null Echo buddy (cosmetic, seen by everyone); Overload It 800 Credits, 600 Renown and crackling pillars in the War-Frame arena (cosmetic). Each choice gives 2 Skill Points once (8 in Act 1 with missions 1 and 6).
- 2026-11-16: M7 reputation: League, Syndicate and City tracks per character, ranks at 100/300/600/1,000/1,500; +5 City per citizen carried to safety anywhere, +50 own side per first mission clear. What ranks unlock waits for the M9 shop and HQ.
- 2026-11-16: M7 side quests: NPCs stand at their anchors (plaza, Old Town, home safehouse for Old Beacon and the Velvet Paw, the Docks) on the nearest open ground; Talk (hold B) starts and pins an arc; only the pinned arc's step is built, for that hero alone, 3 s after the last. All 8 arcs ship (the spec's cut line is the first 4). Skye's reward is a Photo Mode frame (profile.frames), Maribel's cafe sign an HQ prop (profile.props), Pip's Salute an emote bit; the Mayor's Sash and Dock Coat are quest-tier gear styles.
- 2026-11-16: M7 remotes beyond the spec's list: StoryState (the Journal), StoryPin, ChoicePrompt, OpeningState, OpeningAct (Catch, skip the flight), SuitUp, MissionResult, QuestTalk, QuestState. Choice.Pick only answers a prompt the server opened, so a client cannot pick a choice it was not asked.
- 2026-11-16: M7 the cinematic is 4 flat comic panels over 20 s (PLACEHOLDER ART) played when the opening starts, skippable after 2 s; the opening runs under it, so skipping never skips the hand-off or the first incident.
- 2026-11-16: M8 the flag is server-owned (FlagService): on at once from level 10 outside safe areas, at most every 5 s; off only after 60 s with no PvP damage dealt or taken and never inside the Comet Crater. Everyone reads it from player attributes (Pvp, PvpSide, PvpId, Protected, InCrater); the owner gets FlagState on change. Bots never take part in PvP.
- 2026-11-16: M8 safe areas are the HQs (80 studs, as for loadout swaps), the 16 safehouses (radius 40), mission pockets (y 2,900 and up), story scenes and fast-travel flights. Spawn protection is 5 s from any respawn, once per life, ended by any cast.
- 2026-11-16: M8 PvP targets: each hero has a combat slot and a flagged attacker's ability candidates include the flagged heroes it may hit (combat id 65,000 + slot, the aim hint too). Both flags, safe areas, protection and allies are checked again as each hit lands, for every shape (bolts, areas, chains, fields, channels, echoes, combos). Allies (squads) arrive in M10 through FlagService.allyCheck; until then any two flagged players can fight.
- 2026-11-16: M8 stat compression applies to Power (Might) and Health (Vitality), the two stats with a level baseline: the spec formula with level capped at 40. A geared target's damage is scaled so it dies in as many hits as its compressed health would take. Focus, Precision and Resolve have no baseline (the formula divides by 0), so their gear value counts at 25% while flagged (Config.Pvp.minorScale), on the server and in the client's cooldown prediction. Supers deal 60%; Epic set damage bonuses count half; Lucky Strike, Quakeheel and Echo Trail are off; Second Wind and Cooling Kill work at half.
- 2026-11-16: M8 crowd control on players: stuns, roots, slows, knockups and floats live in the hero's Status (stuns and roots with the spec's 8 s halving and 3 s immunity), ride HeroStatus to the owner and stop movement and casting there and on the server. Knockbacks and pulls (max 30 studs) and knockups are a short arc on the target's own client (HeroKnock) that MoveGuard allows. Taunt and blind do nothing to players. Parry Stance blocks a PvP hit and ripostes the attacker.
- 2026-11-16: M8 support rule: an unflagged hero's heals, shields and buffs skip a flagged hero who took or dealt PvP damage in the last 15 s; a flagged helper only reaches one who is an ally. Revives follow the same rule.
- 2026-11-16: M8 Downed (PvE): the humanoid stays up at 1% while the hero is out of play (robots ignore it, no regen, no casting), crawling at 4 studs/s; a Help marker pings every player within 600 studs. Revive is a REVIVE button within 8 studs that needs the helper to stay close and not cast for 3 s (1.5 s Controller); the hero stands with 35% health and the helper gets +40 Renown. Give Up respawns 2 s later; at 10 s the hero is Defeated and respawns after 5 s at the nearest visited safehouse, or the one picked on the screen (Respawn.Choose).
- 2026-11-16: M8 PvP defeat: no Downed state; the hero bursts into sparks (the Overload attribute) and respawns in 5 s. The loser drops 5% of on-hand Credits above 500 (max 300) times the reward factor, so a win that pays nothing also costs the loser nothing. The winner gets that plus Credits 40 + 2 x target level and 15 Fame, times the factor.
- 2026-11-16: M8 anti-farming: wins are logged per server: a third win on one target inside 30 minutes, a target 10+ levels down, pay 0; same side and fights inside a world boss arena pay half (both: a quarter); a 3-and-3 trade inside 60 minutes pays 0 both ways for the hour and writes both accounts to the review queue once.
- 2026-11-16: M8 combat logging: leaving within 15 s of taking PvP damage pays the last attacker as a PvP win (the Credits come off the save before it is written) and leaves an anchored copy of the body in the world for 10 s.
- 2026-11-16: M8 the Comet Crater is the landmark's 400 x 400 square. Walking in starts a 5 s red warning with LEAVE (sets you down 24 studs outside the nearest edge); staying forces PvP for level 10+; below 10 the warning stays and PvP never turns on. Inside, Renown pays +50% (Progress Bonus.renownMult, after the x4 cap).
- 2026-11-16: M8 strikes are one count per account per server (GuardService): a teleport-sized speed fail is a strike (MoveGuard now hands it over), and dropped remote calls count one strike per 10 inside a minute so lag never adds up. 3 strikes in 10 minutes save and kick with "Connection problem. Please rejoin."; the save keeps a kick count (profile.guard) and from the second kick the account goes to the review queue (DataStore CapelineReview_v1, read by hand). No ban exists anywhere. Studio never kicks.
- 2026-11-16: M8 reports: a plate menu on every other player with REPORT (4 preset reasons, no text) and MUTE (hides their chat on this device through TextChatService). One report per reporter per target every 10 minutes; 3 accounts inside 10 minutes put the target in the review queue. Roblox's own Report Abuse stays in the menu.
- 2026-11-16: M8 Fame and Infamy: one bar (char.fame) fed by EconomyService.Earned (incident 5, rescue 2 or 3 for villains, contract 10, mission 40, boss tiers 20/30/50) and PvP wins (15 x factor). Rank rewards: titles Rookie, Local Name, Headliner, Big Deal, Living Legend (title bits for the M9 wheel), the Salute and Bow emotes, the Fame photo frame, 3 Fame-tier styles (Spotlight Visor, Headliner Cape, Legend Gauntlets), the Super Landing pose (emote bit 20) and the gold plate trim (title bit 100). Fame shows on name plates; nothing sold adds Fame.
- 2026-11-16: M8 remotes beyond the spec's list: CraterLeave, DownState, PingShow, PvpResult (the win and defeat card) and HeroKnock. Flag.State is the owner's FlagState plus player attributes for everyone else.
- 2026-11-16: M9 the creator has 11 tabs in the look record's order: Suits, Capes (coats and scarves too), Masks, Gloves (wraps too), Boots, Emblems, Auras, Trails, Voices, plus nate's Hair and Eyes. 146 pieces (37 free, 65 earned in play, 42 sold in 6 packs, 2 in the starter pack); every piece has one account-wide id (1 to 146) and unlocks for every character. Spec said 116 pieces; the anime additions bring the rest.
- 2026-11-16: M9 a look is one 32-byte record (LookCodec: version, the Anime flag, 11 piece indexes, 3 palette colours, hair colour, skin tone) saved as 8 words on the character and on each mannequin. 24 palette colours, 8 skin tones. Comic or Anime is a creator pick and sets the effect style.

- 2026-11-16: fix after nate's first Studio run (map not loading, spawning in the sky and falling): the place starts with Players.CharacterAutoLoads off and the server turns it on after the hub and spawn pads are built; the client GroundGuard holds the hero still (LOADING AREA) after a spawn or any server move until a ray finds streamed-in ground below (12 s cap); server moves stream for up to 5 s first.
- 2026-11-16: second Studio fix: the server boot runs each service's start on its own (one failure is reported and the rest still start), Studio copies every server error into a red box on screen (ReplicatedStorage.ServerErrors, shown by the loading screen), characters load after every service is up, and the Model.LevelOfDetail write in CityBuilder is removed (nate's Studio confirmed it: "cannot write LevelOfDetail (lacking capability PluginOrOpenCloud)", which stopped the whole city from building). Far chunks simply stream out; a pre-built skyline would need the property set in the saved place.
- 2026-11-16: Red Faction-style destruction (nate: "destruction like Red Faction Guerrilla"; nate picked structural chunks on 2026-11-16): every hub building is storeys of 4 corner pillars, wall panels (1 to 4 a face) and floor slabs (Shared.Structure, 18 to 200 pieces, one bit each). A hit knocks out the nearest pieces (one per 600 structure damage, pillars double, at most 10); a storey stands while its pillars and walls keep 35% of their weight, else it and everything above fall; a ground-storey failure is the full collapse. Hanging panels and unheld slab tiles drop. Changes from the spec: "10 to 40 chunks" and "Damaged at 40% / Collapsed at 100% HP" are replaced by these pieces and supports (HP now follows the weight lost); the 3-collapses cap holds the ground storey up instead. Devices draw the holes (the body hides and stops colliding locally) and knocked-out pieces fly as local physics debris inside the preset's debris budget; falling storeys drop as an animated pancake, never shared physics. Server cost stays bits: removed pieces ride the 2 Hz BuildingDiff, the snapshot carries each Damaged building's bits. A Damaged building no longer blocks the server's line of sight; a fallen storey lowers its height for robots (CityBuilder.tops). A robot thrown through a wall breaks 2 panels there.
- 2026-11-16 (nate: "more humanoid, Terminator style ... look like a superhuman race but robotic internals that show as they take more damage"): every robot is an original superhuman android soldier, a synthetic shell (uniform and trim per kind, cartoon skin and hair picked from the robot's id, a diamond chest mark, a cape on the Null Sentinel) over a metal endoskeleton (spine, ribs, glowing core, skull, glowing eyes, bones, joints). The shell breaks off in 15 pieces as health drops (Shared.RobotDamage, Config.Enemies.look): limbs first, then belly and chest, the face last, all gone at 15% health; the order is seeded by the robot's id, so every device agrees with no new network data. Kept original (no copied costume or symbol from any show) and machines only: under the shell is metal, wires and light, never anything else; pieces come off in clean panels with chips and sparks.
- 2026-11-16 (nate, on the class proposal https://claude.ai/artifact/3BRK6wBzD9bo3LDQwmDZWw): classes become 4 archetypes (Elemental, Rogue, Powerhouse, Mystic) with 17 paths, ALL 17 at launch (Storm, Frost, Fire, Earth, Tide; Swordsman, Gunslinger, Gadgeteer, Shadow; Brawler, Martial Artist, Juggernaut, Colossus; Gravity, Mind, Spirit, Light). The 5 existing sets keep their internal ids (Volt = Storm, Titan = Brawler, Pulse = Gravity, Blade = Swordsman, Frost) so saves and combos stay put; only the shown names change. Mostly one path: from level 15 a hero may learn up to 5 abilities from other paths in the same archetype (read as 5 borrowed abilities; the basic and the super stay the main path's). Every hero picks one mobility (Flight, Super-speed, Leaping, Acrobat, Swing) from level 1, swapped free at any safehouse; the old level unlocks (flight 3, super-speed 8, leap 12, grapple 16) are gone. One free archetype or path change before level 10. Underwater comes now, at launch: swimming in the bay and ocean, the Sunken Line, Coral Shelf and Trench, Diver, Anchor and Torpedo Sentry robots, the Leviathan Engine boss, element reactions in water. Names approved.
- 2026-11-16: 17 paths, approximations kept simple for launch (tune after the playtest): Psychic Link is damage resistance plus healing rather than a true damage split; Mech Suit's attacks are a summon field around the hero rather than a separate punch kit; Deadeye Mark is a crit buff rather than a mark on one robot; the Super-speed slide gives no invulnerability frames. A borrowed path's starter abilities cost points like any other (only your main path's starters are free).
- 2026-11-16: underwater is Roblox Terrain water (real swimming and buoyancy) in a 600-stud ring around the coast (4 depth bands, 10 to 42 deep), the harbour basin dug to 36 deep, the Sunken Line tunnel and station under the Docks (reached from the basin floor), and the Trench south of the Coral Shelf (240 deep). Rock walls under the land's edge and the ring's edge keep the water closed; the ground and the quay slab are cut around the basin; sea parts start past the ring with a hole over the Trench. Built by Shared.DeepLayout (pure, tested) and filled in 512-stud chunks a few a frame. Heroes have no air meter (they are supers).
- 2026-11-16: underwater rewards: caches pay Economy.earn.deepCache at the area's zone level (basin 3, line 6, sea 10, shelf 12, trench 25) and roll Loot.sources.deepCache (35% chance of a piece); each hero can open each cache once per 15 minutes. Commuters pay Economy.earn.deepRescue when swum to the quay and come back after 4 minutes. Both are picked up by the server's own position check at 4 Hz, so there is no client remote.
- 2026-11-16: sea robots Diver (typeId 7), Anchor (8) and Torpedo Sentry (9) spawn only near a hero who is underwater (Config.Deep.mix); EnemySim keeps them in the water and keeps walking land robots out of it. The Enemy.Delta packet's typeId grows from 3 to 5 bits (same byte; state keeps its 3). Torpedoes turn after their hero at 60 degrees a second, and the client steers its drawing with the same function.
- 2026-11-16: the Leviathan Engine is the third world boss in the rotation (Rustjaw Prime, Lumen Maw, Leviathan Engine), fought at the surface over the Trench: tail sweep, torpedo volley, whirlpool pull, surge ring, 2 torpedo bays as objectives, eyes and reactor as weak points. Its drop is Leviathan Coils (hands, shiny 1 in 20). The save's boss slots grow to 3 by repair (no version bump: an old save gets 0). The Null factory in the Trench is the Act 2 mission pocket's place; the mission itself comes with Act 2.
- 2026-11-16 (nate: "nothing is destructible as of rn"): before, only supers and the 16 abilities with their own structDmg touched buildings, so a new hero with basics and starter skills broke nothing. Now every damaging ability does: a skill 700 structure damage (at least one piece), a basic 200 that adds up per building (3 basic hits knock out a piece; repair clears it), basics reaching 5 studs around where they land. Only hits worth a whole piece make craters or cracks, scare citizens or flip cars.
- 2026-11-16 (nate: "i want all our assets with blender or at least most"): art is made with Blender 5.0 run headless from Python scripts in art/blender (kit.py helpers, one script per asset set), exported as FBX for Studio's 3D Importer; nate imports and uploads (needs his account). Meshes are generic shapes the game sizes and colours per piece, so one mesh set serves every robot, skin, suit and build. A script also writes Config.ArtFit (how each mesh sits on its piece's box) and renders a preview PNG. The game uses a mesh when ReplicatedStorage.Art has it and falls back to the plain part when not. First set: the robot pieces (38 meshes, about 27k triangles a robot). Next sets after nate approves the look: bosses, gear and creator pieces, summons, vehicles, citizens, buildings and props.
- 2026-11-16: underwater fog works on the sky's Atmosphere (density 0.62 near the surface to 0.85 at the trench floor, tinted), since an Atmosphere overrides Lighting fog.
- 2026-11-16: uploaded meshes live in the repo: the model nate's Studio import created is pulled into art/models/Robots.rbxm (MeshIds kept, see meshids.json) and Rojo maps it to ReplicatedStorage.Art, so builds, rojo serve and the partner all get the meshes without a manual Studio step.
- 2026-11-16 (nate: "All humanoid models should follow that basic r15 roblox model"): every humanoid (heroes, robots, citizens, humanoid bosses) is built on the standard R15 body: 15 MeshParts (Head, UpperTorso, LowerTorso, Upper/Lower arm, Hand, Upper/Lower leg, Foot each side) with standard Motor6Ds and attachments, so Roblox animations, emotes, accessories and layered clothing work and one animation set serves all. Same day nate refined it: "r15 is right but i want the blocky version like r6": the classic blocky proportions (2 x 2 x 1 torso, 1-wide limbs cut in three, the classic head), softly bevelled. Robots keep the breakable shell (a shell piece per R15 part over the endoskeleton) and stay client-drawn without Humanoid physics (AnimationController) for speed. Non-humanoid bosses (Leviathan Engine) stay custom. AI-generated models (Meshy/Tripo, often 100k-500k triangles) are reduced in Blender to budget (hero ~10k triangles over 15 parts, robot ~8k; Roblox caps a mesh at 20k), with detail baked into normal maps.

Studio on nate's desktop tops out near 49 fps even on an empty baseplate, so Studio fps is not the game's fps; Capeline costs ~8 ms a frame over empty. - 2026-11-17: hero animations (overhaul plan step 2) are code-driven: hand-keyed clips as data (Shared.HeroAnim) played by HeroAnimator, which writes Motor6D.Transform in PreSimulation after the Animator, so they need no uploads and work on any R15 rig. Roblox's default Animate keeps the ground walk, run, idle and jump. A clip moves only the joints it lists (casts keep the flight legs). Other devices pose you from a new MoveMode remote (the server sets a Move attribute, looks only, never trusted by MoveGuard). Real animations can replace the clips later without touching the callers.
- 2026-11-17: the effects library (overhaul plan step 3) adds textured particles under CombatFx's part-built shapes rather than replacing them: per element (13 elements from the 17 sets plus weapons), pooled emitters moved and Emit-ed per effect, a particles-a-second budget and a share per device preset (Config.Fx.lib, rules in Shared.FxPlan). Built-in Roblox particle images now; 10 generated sheets (art/fx/textures.py) wait for nate's upload, then only ids change. Hit-stop now also holds HeroAnimator's pose, and every non-tick hit gives a small camera FOV kick (Combat.punchHit / punchHeavy). Still to do in step 3: hold-to-aim and charge controls and one remade robot on one Downtown block, after nate judges this look.
- 2026-11-17: hold to aim and charge (overhaul plan step 3; Config.Combat.charge, Shared.ChargeRules). Skills and supers show their aim while held and cast on release (a tap still casts at once, on release); the basic is unchanged. Only damaging aimed skills charge (bolt, line, chain, cone, arc, nova; not channels, buffs, fields, summons, dashes, rides or supers): 0.25 s to 1.2 s held gives up to +20% damage and +15% size, a modest gain since the cooldown starts later. The server times the hold itself from a CombatCharge remote at the press and caps the claimed charge by it (plus latency and 0.15 s leeway), so nobody can claim more than they held. Keys stay Z X C V / Q E R T for skills and the existing super key; F stays Fly.
- 2026-11-17: the remade robot (overhaul plan step 3) is the Spark Trooper, the most common robot: a full armour shell set on the blocky R15 body (Art.Trooper, 15 parts that fill their R15 boxes so every build and joint still fits, plus shoulder pads and a back thruster as extras), ~6,600 triangles, one 512 px texture set (colour, normal, roughness, metalness). The colour map is see-through on the suit (SurfaceAppearance Overlay), so the part colour still drives the suit, the hit flash and the ice tint, and the same set can dress other robots later. The helmet replaces the face and hair (an android trooper, no skin); the eyes become a glowing visor slit and the chest mark a glowing core lamp. Falls back to the old look until imported.

Skin and hair are picked per robot again. ~5,000 triangles, same texture set and Overlay tinting. Every later robot follows this rule.

- 2026-11-17 (overnight; nate: "Dont want enemies to get repetitive", then go on all 9 proposed types): nine new robot types, each with its own switch (`on = true` in Config.Enemies; off means it never spawns anywhere): Shield Bearer (a guard whose riot shield blocks every front hit and cuts front hits on robots right behind it by 60%; a flank hit drops it; its bash slows), Captain (hangs back 22 to 36 studs, rallies robots within 35 studs every 9 s for +25% damage and +20% speed; when it falls they are slowed 3 s; an ambient one brings 2 escorts), Sniper (keeps 50 to 120 studs, 1.6 s laser on one hero, aim locks for the last 0.35 s, one 3x round; a stun breaks the aim), Bomber (grenades land 1.1 s after a ring shows, 7-stud blast, then a 4 s burning patch; never at fliers), Medic Unit (repairs the most damaged robot within 28 studs 8% a second from behind it, never another Medic), Swarmer (packs of 6 small robots, 35 HP, surround, lunge and nip; each counts a third for the 6-on-one cap), Mimic (dressed as a citizen and not a hit target until a hero is 12 studs away or it is hit; near a fight 5 s it walks up at a citizen's pace; 1.8x ambush strike), Interceptor (a fast flyer that only hunts heroes 10+ studs in the air; a net every 7 s roots 0.7 s so the hero drops out of flight), Grappler (a hook 10 to 50 studs pulls a hero, fliers too, to 6 studs from it and down, then holds 0.9 s; then punches). They join open-world squads (incidents, contracts, events, side quests through SiteKit.squad) by swapping in by level (Shared.EnemyVariety, Config.Enemies.variety: Swarmer from 3 up to Mimic from 9), so level 1 fights stay the classic robots; story missions and the opening keep their set robots. Crowd control on heroes uses the PvP rules (HeroService.cc: repeats shorten). Wire: Enemy.Delta rows are 13 bytes (an extra flags byte), 7 new CombatEvents kinds. Fast shots are tested along the whole step (no tunnelling). Damage at level 10 sits between the Sneak Bot and the Sentry (Swarmer pack about 70 a second if all six nip).
- 2026-11-17 (overhaul plan step 8, the phone pass): every screen is laid out from one pure plan, Shared.TouchLayout (Config.Ui.phone): the action cluster in rings around the Basic (Basic 84, skills 60, super 64, Leap, Fly, Boost, Down, Gadget, Swap, Awaken; a Block slot kept for H1) scaled to fit under the top row and in 60% of the width (at least 44 px for the main buttons, 40 for the rest), times the new Button size setting (80 to 130%, key 11, added last so the Settings.Set numbers stay); mouse and gamepad draw it at 85%. Spec change: on touch the spec's "bottom 80 px right (the flight pad)" clear strip becomes the 16 px safe margin, because Roblox's jump button is hidden (JumpPower 0) and the cluster cannot fit 317 px of iPhone SE height otherwise; the camera pad is the rest of the right side. On a phone (short side under 500) the radar (88 px, no taps: MAP opens the map), status card, wallet, buffs and up to 2 tracker rows share the top-left corner, all display, so the move stick works under them; a touch tablet puts the radar beside the status card. The top-row buttons from every module (POWERS, GEAR, MAP, SHOP, PING, PVP, JOURNAL, SQUAD, EMOTE, PHOTO, the gear) go in one shared Kit dock that fills by order and puts the rest in a MORE drawer. Panels use Kit.fit (a UIScale down to 0.8 so they fit the safe area; lists scroll); landscape phones keep the creator's three columns with slimmer chrome. Hold-to-aim on touch drags the camera (InputController.holdTouch), the charge shows as a ring and cooldowns as seconds. Gamepad: Select is now Menu (it opens the top row and selects its first button; Gear stays on I and the top row), and X also catches in the opening. Headless check: tests/uisim.luau runs the real UI modules from the built place at iPhone SE, iPhone 14, a 20:9 Android, iPad and 1080p (plus left-handed) and fails on off-screen, overlapping, tiny or thumb-blocked controls.

Only PCs and consoles step up to high on the frame test. After it, a watch steps one preset down when fps stays under 26 for 20 s (never up; Auto only). New preset fields: citizensDrawn (low 30 nearest of the 60, medium and high 60), citizen pose distance and rate (low 60 m / every 4 frames; medium and high keep 100 / 3), bloom and sunRays (off on low). The Renown curve keeps 1.12 a level to 40 (68,400, unchanged) and then grows 3% a step (Economy.levels.lateGrowth): 40 to 50 is 87,590 more (155,989 in all), so a level keeps about the same hours as the late 30s. 1,000-hero sim: the 10-hour targets hold (levels 21 to 26, build 12.8 h); median 46.5 h to 40 (unchanged) and 91.5 h to 50 (41 to 50 adds about 45 h, 4.5 h a level). PLACEHOLDER LOOK: boss lore pending (nate, 2026-11-17: world and event bosses stand apart from the main story, are not the villain's machines, and their lore is not decided), so the looks are generic cartoon machines in neutral greys and steel with industrial hazard stripes: no faction emblems, no text, no story looks, no colour that names a faction; the shapes only follow each fight. Each boss is 7 meshes by role: hull, plates that break off at phase 2 and at phase 3 (a visible phase change on top of the banner), Neon accents in one shared per-phase colour (cool blue, orange, red-hot), Neon weak points in ONE pulsing gold on every boss ("hit the gold"; each lamp sits inside its Config.Boss hit sphere, tested), and the objective body plus its red lamp at each objective. Looks only: hit boxes, HP, attacks and timings unchanged. 6,000 to 12,000 triangles a boss (budget 16,000, 10,000 a mesh). Flight ceiling raised from soft 560 / hard 600 to 920 / 960 and the cloud layer from 500 to 880 (spec numbers), so there are 100 studs of sky over the tallest spire. Window bands thin out above 144 studs (every 36), so no building has more than 32 parts (avg ~9); Downtown 2,593 parts, Uptown 1,191, hub boxes 5,421 at seed 202647; with StreamingEnabled a phone holds only what is near.
- 2026-11-17: every power path has its own look (overhaul plan step 6), built on the effects library the same way as the Volt slice and drawn over the same shapes, so the slice and weapon moves are unchanged. Each path's row in Config.Fx.paths names a head that flies (bolts, chain hops, lines), a hit mark, a flourish on the caster, a dash trail, what drifts in its fields, charge motes that orbit the hand while a skill charges or a windup builds (Shared.FxPlan.orbit), and a super moment of its own (16 looks: ice crown, falling sun, vines, a wave wall, a giant crescent, a tracer fan, plates locking on, an eclipse, a crater, a ringed beam, anchors, pillars, a black hole, a ground sigil, a spirit column, a sunrise). - 2026-11-17 (robots review): fixed in the nine new robot types: a Pull now breaks a Sniper's aim like a stun (before, the round fired the moment the pull ended, with no laser); a stun or a fall cuts a Grappler's hook in flight (before, a fallen or stunned Grappler's hook still yanked the hero, with the client's hook already gone); an Interceptor nets only a hero in the air (it keeps shooting a landed hero for its 2 s grace, but no net); a site's Mimic shows itself once no other robot of its fight is within 150 studs (Config.Enemies.Mimic.disguise.alone), so a hero hanging back or hovering can always finish the site; a Swarmer's pack and a Captain's escort never spawn on a roof (a building spot starts on the leader's); the hook's MoveGuard grant is its flat pull + 10 (the drop is a fall, free in MoveCheck, so granting it only banked budget), via EnemySim.hookPull; the Sniper's laser, the red screen edge, the lock sound and the Medic's beam end on the client when the robot's aim or mend bit drops; the Dev panel's robot button skips switched-off types (EnemySim.nextEnabled). Tests in tests/run.luau ("New robot types (review)").
- 2026-11-17 (robots review): speed: tests/bench_enemies.luau (150 robots, 30 heroes, 60 s at 60 Hz, hits and stuns, deltas for every hero): all 18 kinds 0.30 ms a step vs 0.27 ms for the old 9 (1.12x), so no hot path was changed; Interceptor the costliest alone (0.44 ms a step for 150). Enemy.Delta in that scene is 231 KB/s for 30 heroes (7.7 KB/s each: 60 drawn robots x 13 bytes x 10 Hz). Balance: tests/balance_enemies.luau (one robot vs a standing default-class hero, no gear): at level 50 the old kinds take 5.6 s (Sentry) to 20 s (Torpedo Sentry); the new ones 6.5 s (Swarmer pack, by design about a Sentry), 8.6 s (Bomber, only if the hero stands in the fire), 10 to 24 s for the rest, Medic 36 s and one Swarmer 39 s (support and pack roles). No numbers changed.
- 2026-11-17 (more activities): incidents 10 -> 24 templates (Config.Incidents.order) with 6 new step kinds in Shared.IncidentPlan: nest (robots on a tall roof, IncidentSpots finds roofs 30-150 high), leader (down the Captain, the escort may stay), find (the Mimic in a crowd of bystanders), convoy (stop the Medic Units' car before it gets away), barrage (block the Bomber's shells in cover rings, 3 misses fails) and tug (down the Grappler before it tows the car off). A template that features a new robot type `requires` it: skipped while the type's `on` switch is off or before its unlock level (Config.Enemies.variety.swaps); a squad row may name a fallback type instead (`{kind, min, max, fallbackKind, fallbackMin, fallbackMax}`). New step beats (Toast id "beat") play an existing Juice sound to heroes within 150 studs.
- 2026-11-17 (more activities): events 6 -> 12 (Airship drop, Bridge siege, Stadium lockdown, Power plant overload, Parade gone wrong, Harbour fog). Each has a `goal` the server builds generically and a `where` resolved by name through Shared.EventPlan.areas: "downtown" is every district whose name holds "downtown" or that is listed in Config.Events.downtowns ({"Downtown", "Uptown"}, so the second downtown on enemies9 joins with no code change), a list names districts or World.landmarks, and "hub"/"anchor" keep the old behaviour. No coordinates in event data. Harbour fog's look is client only (ActivityFx: fog banks plus a ColorCorrection, Lighting fog left to DeepClient).
- 2026-11-17 (more activities; spec number change): contracts 6 -> 12 types (Bounty, Demolition, Rooftop courier, Defusal, Photo recon, Generator hold) and the board offers 4 a week instead of 3 (Config.Contracts.offers; spec said 3) so the bigger pool shows. Bounty names come from a fixed list of 12 (no free text) and the target type falls back to the first switched-on, unlocked one (Brute Bot last). Photo recon counts a Photo Mode shot (Telemetry kind 4) via the new Analytics.Photo signal. Defusal is hero only.
- 2026-11-17 (more activities): Heroic tier (Config.Heroic, pure rules Shared.HeroicRules, tested): from level 40 an incident rolls Heroic 25%, an event 30%, a contract offer 34%; robots +5 levels, +1 robot in the first squad row, x1.5 Credits and Renown, gear drops at level +2 (capped at Stats.levelCap), 15% more time, a purple HEROIC tag on the tracker, toasts and board. Economy check (HeroicRules.incomeBump over Economy.sim.per30 at level 45): Credits +10.4%, Renown +7.2% over the whole play model, so 40 -> 50 is about 7% shorter (roughly 45 h to 42 h); bosses, side quests and the shop are untouched. Tune chance or rewardMult if that is too quick.
- 2026-11-17 (store art, notes/Store Page.md): the icon and thumbnail candidates are rendered with our own pipeline (headless Blender bpy, the blocky R15 hero in our classic clothes, the remade Spark Trooper, the city kit and its JSON layouts) and finished in PIL (art/store/*.py, `bash art/store/make.sh`), not an image generator, so there is no generator licence to check and every pixel is our own IP.

- 2026-11-17 (store art): thumbnail 3's caption is "GIANT WORLD BOSS" instead of "20-MINUTE WORLD BOSS", because Config.Boss.schedule.every is 1200 to 1800 s (20 to 30 minutes), so "20-minute" would be a claim the game doesn't keep.
- 2026-11-17 (store art): the captions use one original style (heavy italic capitals, a white or gold gradient, an ink outline and a hard drop shadow) in Inter Black Italic (SIL Open Font License); thumbnail 1 is "FLY. FIGHT. SAVE.", thumbnail 2 "HERO OR VILLAIN?", thumbnail 3 "GIANT WORLD BOSS"; the icons have no text.

Picking an origin puts its crest on the Symbol slot (changeable). Never a stat (a test checks origin rows hold only text). A new hero must pick one; any hero changes it free in the creator (origin_pick event).

HeroCreate gains an optional last arg, the origin.

Titles are Claude's picks; nate may rename them in Config.Enemies.<Kind>.fake.title.

The shell stays whole until then, so nothing gives the robot away early; after that it breaks off over the rest of the health like any robot's (RobotDamage). The plate shows the hero name and title with no robot tag, then FAKE in red over the name.

- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 4): balance band, measured per (20 + 4 x level) base damage: fakes and every twin style do 0.66 to 0.70 per second at levels 5, 20, 40 and 50. That is above the strongest elite (Captain 0.61) and below the hardest-hitting world boss (Rustjaw Prime 0.74). The Lumen Maw hits softer than the elites, so it is not the bound. tests/run.luau "Lore 4" and tests/balance_enemies.luau check it.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 4): the evil twin (EnemyService.spawnTwin(player, pos, level)) copies the player's active character's LookCodec words exactly, and its path. Six fighting styles cover the 17 paths: blaster (Volt, Frost, Fire, Tide, Pulse, Mind, Spirit, Light), lobber (Earth, Gadgeteer), gunner (Gunslinger), duelist (Blade), striker (Shadow), smasher (Titan, Martial, Juggernaut, Colossus). Its attacks use that path's CombatFx look and the player's power colour. Its plate reads the hero name (or the display name) and origin title, never @name. The look reaches clients by a new S2C remote, EnemyIdentity, which is sent again to players who join later and cleared on death. Twins are never pooled.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 4): wire: EnemyBuffer EXTRA bit 64 = unmasked, shot style 4 = a path shot (CombatFx.pathShot), CombatEvents 15 = dash and 16 = unmask. The cracked face decals Face_<Name>_Cracked.png from art/clothes/faces.py go in Config.NpcLooks.faces.<Name>.cracked; 0 means the part-built crack overlay shows until nate uploads them. New DEV commands fake 1-3 and twin have panel buttons; the 3x robot button skips the fakes.
- 2026-11-17: Art ids: Open Cloud gives Decal ids, but clothes, faces, particles and MaterialVariant maps need the image id inside each decal, and MaterialVariant maps can only be set by Studio/plugins (PluginSecurity), not game scripts. So nate runs art/upload/resolve_ids.lua once in Studio's command bar (it reads each decal's image id) and pastes the result; art/upload/apply_ids.py writes the image ids into NpcLooks, Creator and Fx configs and builds the 22 city MaterialVariants as src/materials/*.model.json in MaterialService. Image ids baked at build time; no runtime lookups.

Each side's first member (the leader on the leader's side) answers that side's choice.

Each pays 2 Skill Points once, 100 reputation (own side or City) and records its story flag (StoryPlan.flags); Reprogram It gives the Echo Drone (cosmetic), Destroy It makes Live's pillars crackle (cosmetic), Test Her adds a guard ambush, and Framed's picks choose the next step (the runaway train, chasing the cracked Radiant, or the cash van). - 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 5): pockets: Framed gets a new bridge layout (deck over water, two towers, rescue rings, trucks, the train line, the cash van, a chase over the towers); the dungeon becomes the tunnels to the safehouse door with 3 scanner relays; the tower gets the Syndicate's relay; the rooftops get the copy's roof; the arena gets a stage, a giant screen, 3 cameras and 3 stage screens. The station layout is unused by Act 1 now and kept. Fixed an old bug on the way: trainDir was stored as a place, so the train ran in a wrong direction.
- 2026-11-17 (nate: "Make hits weighty": flash, turn enemy white, sound, particles, knockback, shake and freeze): every non-tick hit you land now flashes the robot white for 0.1 s (clothes nearly hidden), freezes you AND the robot for the hit-stop (0.06 s, 0.11 s on crits and heavy hits, was 0.03/0.05), then the robot recoils away from the hit while the server shoves it 1.5 studs (4 on heavy/crit; not ticks, not knockback-immune robots), sparks spray out of the hit, a thud plus metal clank plays (Config.Audio.impact, PLACEHOLDER SOUND), and the camera shakes on every hit (bigger on heavy/crit). All numbers in Config.Combat.weight. Reduce flashing turns off shake and freeze.
- 2026-11-17 (nate: longer prologue chase): the Ceremony prologue's chase is 6 roofs on a purpose-built rooftop row (Config.City.downtown.row; CityLayout Layout.row): six designed buildings (heights 24 to 44, rises at most 8, gaps 38 in a block and 64 across the street, all inside one Leap) on the north lot strip of core blocks (4,3) and (5,3) east of the Hall; those 4 lots keep their draws, so no other building moves; the open squares, the Hall and the safehouse lots are untouched; +16 Downtown parts (2,593 to 2,609); 2 more first-Downtown buildings means 2 fewer Old Town houses in the 220 wreckable hub list. With the row the stage faces it and the spark point is the street spot (of 24 tries) nearest its first roof; without the row the route falls back to today's 3-roof chain (roofs.fallback). Config.Story.opening: roofs.count 3 -> 6, stuck.chase 60 -> 90 s (timeout stays 330), two more fixed Patch chase lines by side.
- 2026-11-17 (nate asked to build the Hero MMO features H1 to H8): H1 block keys: the spec's F stays Fly (2026-11-17 charge decision), so Block is Alt on PC; LB on a gamepad as the spec says, which moves Skill 3 to d-pad left and Skill 4 to d-pad down (POWERS loses d-pad down and opens from its HUD button); the phone shield button sits above FLY (above LEAP), and AWAKEN moved up to make room.
- 2026-11-17: H1 rules the spec left open: a perfect block beats breakers too (timing wins) but never a grab; a shatter from Guard running out takes the hit in full like a breaker; a block needs 10 Guard to start; no block inside a safehouse (HQ keeps it for the training dummies); robots' light hits (coefficient under 0.8: Spark Trooper, Sentry, Sneak Bot front stab) interrupt heroes too, the spec's "alike"; an interrupted charge costs half its cooldown, like an interrupted wind-up.
- 2026-11-17: H1 robot blocks live in BlockService, not EnemySim (the enemy thread is reworking EnemySim): Brute Bots raise a blue block for 2.2 s every 7 s while fighting (first after 2 s, 70% less from the front 200 degrees, a breaker shatters it: 1 s stun, no block for 5 s); a breaker from the front also breaks the Null Sentinel's guard. Brute slams and Sentinel sweeps are breakers against a hero's block. The comic words pop every time (at most every 0.4 s per hero), not only the first time. Juice gets 4 rows (block, perfectBlock, shatter, interrupt; the spec's 31 stay first).
- 2026-11-17 (coordinator relaying nate): event and world bosses and most side quests are standalone from the main story; no Hero MMO feature ties to the old story lore (generic robots and places, placeholders where story text would go).
- 2026-11-17: H2 charge-up powers are 24 existing damaging skills, 1 or 2 per path so all 17 paths and 4 archetypes have one (Volt Chain Lightning, Ground Spark; Frost Shatter, Frost Nova; Fire Whip; Mud Wave; Geyser; Crescent Wave, Rising Blade; Trick Shot, Ricochet; Grapple Yank; Chain Sickle; Ground Slam, Haymaker; Ki Blast, Dragon Rise; Shockwave Stomp; Boulder Fist; Crush, Repulse; Telekinesis; Soul Chains; Prism Beam). Each row carries charge = { max 1.2 s, 2.2x damage, 1.5x size }; full is a breaker. They extend the hold-to-charge (Shared.ChargeRules, the same CombatCharge timing and cap); every other aimed skill keeps the modest +20% charge and is never a breaker or cut off. Charging slows the hero to 60% (Config.Combat.charge.powerSlow) and shows others a glow (the Charging attribute).
- 2026-11-17: H3 Grab key: the spec's E is Skill 2, so Grab is B (the existing interact key; the opening's catch step keeps it while it runs), a tap on the GRAB prompt on phones, and B on a controller while on the ground (B is Down only in the air); throwing is the attack button along the aim, as the spec says. Grab again sets it down at any time (not only standing still).
- 2026-11-17: H3 throwables: up to 80 plain-part props on Downtown's inner north-south sidewalks (2 a side a block, fixed seed, skipped where something already stands or off the street level), plus the hub's cars and trucks (VehicleService) as heavy things; trams are not liftable (34 studs, on lines; they keep the flip). Downed robots wait on the enemy thread. Carrying blocks casting and Block; a carried thing is welded overhead (massless, no collisions) so it rides with the hero's own movement, and a throw is a scripted parabola every client flies from ThrowFly (the spec's "server arc", no free physics). Landings hit through CombatService (the same candidates, so only heroes flagged against the thrower), knock nearby props over, and heavy ones blast the Structure grid (1,200, which also flips nearby cars).
- 2026-11-17: H4 sidekicking applies the raise to stats, health and gear item levels (each worn piece below the raised level counts at it, its rolled stats scaled by the item level ratio), only while hero.scene is "mission" (HeroService.statLevel), and MissionService ends it when the member leaves the pocket. The mission level (the top member, never below the mission floor) replaces the median for pockets only; pocket robots and bosses get more health per player (Config.Sidekick.partyHp 1, 1.6, 2.2, 2.8), as the spec's "use the mission level and the party size".
- 2026-11-17: H4 task forces run inside MissionService as chained legs (each leg a mission-shaped table on an existing pocket layout); "within 2 levels of the task force's floor" reads as: starting one needs level 12, joining a squad (board or Looking) needs level 10 or more and sidekicking raises you; START takes every squad member wherever they are; rejoining works on the same server only; the weekly double Renown is saved per character as mmo.tfWeek (the spec's "taskForce: not saved" stays true for the run itself). Pay is 3x an incident reward at each helper's own level (helpers: squadmates within 60 studs).

- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Sunken City): the Underwater chain, built from nate's draft ("an ancient sunken city wakes up, and its guardian wants the surface gone; the Tidewright, the city's last guardian"), 8 quests, chain save bit 8, title "Tide Turner" (title bit 12) and the Coral Crest head style (n 27). Claude's fills where the doc is silent: the city's name Velmara, its old air dome (so its pocket is walked, not swum), the four tide engines that raise the wave, the salvage drill (the dredger) that woke it, Doc Pearl the marine scientist at the Reef Station (the giver), Dace the salvage diver lost in the kelp, and the unlock at level 12 (the Coral Shelf's zone floor) after the Safehouse.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Sunken City): deep sites: a side quest site with `deep = "shelf"` (ReefStation, DrownedGate, TideSpire) is a seabed spot picked by rule from Shared.DeepLayout (SideQuestSites.deep: a grid search round `near` for water `depth` deep, `away` from every sunken cache, commuter rescue, the Leviathan Engine's arena, every reef, kelp stand and wreck and the shelf's sides, and from the deep sites before it by id). The underwater world is fixed, so the pick is cached once per Config and never reads world.tilePlans. Goals at a deep site go in the open water (deepSpot, deepPath: the Coral Shelf or the open sea ring only, never the basin, the Sunken Line, the Trench or the arena); only the kinds in Config.SideQuests.deep.kinds are allowed there.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Sunken City): no new enemy type (typeIds 1 to 31 are full on the wire): the Tidewright is chain 6's finale dress (Config.SideQuests.dress, EnemyService.dress, the EnemyIdentity wire), extended so a dress with `robot` may go over a plain finale robot (no costumed suit swims), drawn like a costumed villain (never cracks or reads FAKE) in its `path`'s look, with `height`, a `trident` prop and `dmgMult` (1 to 2). He is the sea brute Anchor in his dress: health 700 x 10 (Brine's 2200 x 3 band), damage x 1.6 (between the elite robots and the world bosses). Built first as a separate `guise` while chain 6 was in flight, then folded into the dress at the merge so there is one way to dress a villain. Sea robots only in the chain (Diver, Anchor, Torpedo Sentry), and no Diver in the air dome, since a sea flyer is kept in the water.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Sunken City): the Velmara pocket (PocketLayout "velmara") follows the sea cave's shape: a terrace 20 studs up over an engine hall, a 9-step stair between them, the hall's ceiling tagged. New speakers tidewright and dace, portrait extra goggles, comic pictures velmara, flood, guardian and dredger.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Pit): the Old Quarry chain, built from nate's draft ("an underground fight club for powered brawlers, run by a crime family"; the Pitboss), 8 quests. Claude's fills where the doc is silent: the chain is called The Pit; the crime family is the Gravel family, who kept the shut quarry's mine tunnels and dug the Pit under the floor; the Pitboss is the family's eldest son, whose Spark turned his skin to quarry stone, and he fights in a powered prize-fighter rig; the fights are rigged and a fighter who loses owes the family and must fight again; the giver is Brick, a retired Pit fighter at the quarry gate; Flint is the young fighter who runs (a guide speaker); the Vigil free the fighters, photograph the bouncer bots and smash the rigged odds machines; the Syndicate rob the collectors, sell the betting book to a rival, crack the odds machines and empty the vault. Standalone: no Crane. Names and lines wait on nate's OK.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Pit): no new robot type (Enemy.Delta's typeId has 5 bits and 1 to 31 are used). The chain's robots are existing types (BurglarBot, BruteBot, SneakBot, ShieldBearer); the Pitboss is a new framework piece, a dress: Config.SideQuests.dress.Pitboss (a creator look, name, title, the suit's 4 robot colours, creature = false) worn by an existing costumed suit, the Bramblebeast (brute brain: slam, shockwave, charge; Earth path), hpMult 2. FakeRules.dress builds it; EnemyService.dress sends it as the robot's EnemyIdentity (the evil twin's wire, late joiners too); EnemyRenderer builds that robot for its dress, never pooled, and it sparks apart like a machine. The validator checks every dress and that it goes only on a fake.costumed finale enemy. The Pitboss depends on Config.Enemies.Bramblebeast.on.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Pit): the sites stand on the Old Quarry landmark itself (Config.SideQuests.sites with `quarry`, Shared.SideQuestSites.quarry), read from WorldGen.landmark's own boxes: the landmark is hand-placed on fixed tiles, so the sites never move with the seed, need no tile plans and need no SideQuestSites.warm. QuarryGate: the middle of the 80-stud gap in the north wall, facing town. MineLift and QuarryRing: the 4-stud floor point nearest a spot (-60, -110 and 0, 75 from the middle) that keeps 10 and 14 studs from every wall, terrace and boulder. The finale on the quarry floor shares the Lumen Maw arena, accepted the way other chains sit near the bosses.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, The Pit): its inside is a new pocket layout "pit" (a rock gallery 20 studs up with the mine tunnel, the bookies' room, the betting hall and 4 odds machines; rock steps down to the sand pit floor; a ring on a 2-stud stage with glowing ropes, floodlights, bench tiers and the Pitboss's box seat; under 400 parts). Unlock after Act 1's Safehouse at level 20 (the Old Quarry's zone floor). Reward: the title "Undisputed" (story title bit 11) and the Undisputed Belt belt style (Story tier, belt n = 29). Chain save bit 7. Scenes: quarryNight 125, pitStory (comic page) 126, fightNight 127; that fills Config.Cinema.extraBits (120 to 127), so the next chain's scenes need the seen set widened (ProfileSchema) first.
- 2026-11-18: the Sunken City's salvage diver is Dace (was Marlo, which is already a Mossbell Stand hero); speaker id dace.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, character lines): the four character lines from nate's lore doc, one chain each (layer "character", taken only from the giver in person: unlock.npc): The Last Partner (6 quests, chain save bit 9), Finding Lark (7, bit 10), Hostile Takeover (6, bit 11, villains only) and The Scoop (6, bit 12). Titles True North, Found Family, Fireproof and Front Page (title bits 13 to 16); styles Twin Star Cape (back n 39), Relay Visor (head n 38), Scorchproof Gauntlets (hands n 37), Press Vest (chest n 36), counted down from 39 to keep clear of other helpers counting up. Scenes 160 to 171 (Cinema.wideBits). No new robot types.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, character lines): Claude's fills where the doc is silent: Lodestar's partner is Vesper, the Evening Star, who died in the harbour fire last winter and came back last month as a famous stranger (a dress over the famous fake Radiant, so she cracks and reads FAKE; who built her is left open); Patch's sister is Lark, a flier taken by Catchpole the Spark catcher to the Spark Pound in the Docks; the Syndicate boss is Duchess Vanta of the Opal Room in Old Town and her rival Sal Scorch of the Torch Crew ("removed" is beaten and run off, cartoon style); the reporter is Nora Bright of BHN 24 (the news channel's existing name) against the Censor, a masked man who cuts the news. All names are nate's to approve or rename.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, character lines): unlocks: The Last Partner after mission 4 at level 8, Finding Lark after mission 3 at level 6, Hostile Takeover after mission 2 at level 6, The Scoop after mission 6 at level 10 (the story beats each character has by then; the levels sit over each district's floor). Each line's finale is a dress (Config.SideQuests.dress) over an existing suit on the finale list: Radiant (Vesper), the Harvest King (Catchpole), Redline (Scorch) and Masque-a-Rade (the Censor), each dress's build matching the suit's.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, character lines): sites by rule in the city's existing buildings: Downtown (the Night Owl, BHN 24 Studios) and Uptown (Vesper's Penthouse) by the academy's and bank's rules; Docks and Old Town buildings (new: Shared.SideQuestSites districtBuilding) as the one nearest `near` from the district's middle within a height range, away.pad from every safehouse pad and away.door from both HQ doors, with a street door on open ground. Every new site keeps `away.site` (200 to 250 studs) from every other building or square site (of two that both ask, the later id keeps it), so the chains never share a building.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, character lines): framework additions: a dress with `famous` goes over a famous fake (not a costumed suit or the twin) and cracks and reads FAKE like one; a dressed fake's crack fires no Unmasked news (the headline would name the suit's hero). A chain with unlock.side is checked for that side only (goals, lines, blurb), and the service skips a step with no goal for the player's side. A giver with `relay` (a "radio" giver, no look) is drawn as a relay box (Patch is radio only), and any giver may have its own `none` line, one per side (Vanta to a Vigil hero).
