Hostile Takeover keeps every goal on the villain key and its lines as plain `text`; Lark, Catchpole, Scorch, the Censor and Vesper are new guide speakers; Vanta and Nora speak as givers.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, origin lines): the five origin lines from nate's lore doc, one chain each (layer "origin", offered only to heroes of that origin: unlock.origin, Config.Creator.origins 1 to 5; other origins never see or get offered them, and SideQuestPlan.allDone asks for the hero's own line only), all after the Safehouse (mission 2) at level 5, 6 quests each: The Ashgrove Test (Lab, chain save bit 13), Starblood Calling (Alien, bit 14), The Last Owner (Magic, bit 15), Suit Recall (Tech, bit 16) and The Taken Master (Training, bit 17). Titles Test Passed, Starborn, Relic Keeper, No Returns and Lesson Learned (title bits 17 to 21) and the Story styles Kindling Core (chest), Starfall Wings (back), Moonstone Circlet (head), Clawback Gauntlet (hands) and Master's Belt (belt), each style n 32. Scenes: wide seen bits 192 to 201.
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, origin lines): Claude's fills where the doc is silent (nate to approve): the lab is Ashgrove Labs (its Kindler, a ring of light, woke a Lab-Born hero's Spark; Doctor Holloway, its director with no Spark, tests volunteers to take one; giver Dr. Okafor, the night-shift scientist); the alien signal is a song from an old pod on Radio Hill, the ship a Star-Blooded hero's ancestor came down in, and the Voidhound is an ancient hunting machine that followed it (giver Radio Ruth); the relic's last owner is Ambrose the Astonishing, a stage magician who vanished forty years ago, hunted by his jealous rival Morrow the Mirage (giver Auntie Moth, who sold the relic); the suit's dead scrapyard core lit up when its Suit-Forged maker strapped in, and Clawback the repo king wants it back (giver Tinker Tess, whose garage built it); the old master is Master Hoshi of the Willow Street Dojo, taken by Gale, his angriest old student whose Spark never woke (giver Kiko, his youngest student). Each line's step 5 tells its truth on both sides (a comic page ending on the same panel: every powered person has one Spark, the origin only woke it).
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, origin lines): finales are dresses over existing robots (no new enemy type): Holloway over the Warden (NullSentinel) and the Voidhound over the Captain and Clawback over the Grappler as robot dresses (Light, Pulse and Gadgeteer looks; damage lifted 1.4, 1.1 and 1.1 into the boss-lite band), Morrow over Madame Masque-a-rade's suit and Gale over Redline's. Health 5400 to 7200 (2 to 4 Captain Brines like the other chains). Two pockets: "ashlab" (reception, records, the test chamber, stairs down to the sublevel's four Kindlers) and "ironhall" (locker room, trophy room, the training floor, stairs down to Hoshi's cage on four chains).
- 2026-11-17 (nate's lore, build plan step 11, origin lines): sites by rule: ASHGROVE LABS a Downtown building south-east of the plaza and THE MOTH & MOON CURIOS an Old Town building (the character lines' rules; ids Underlab and VintageCurios sort after their sites so no earlier pick moves), RADIO HILL's first terrace on its town side (new: Shared.SideQuestSites.hill reads the hand-placed landmark's boxes; district "RadioHill"), and two Suburbs lots like Tick-Tock Toys (WILLOW STREET DOJO a shop, TINKER'S GARAGE a house). Every existing chain's site and race course is unchanged across weeks (tested). Names checked against nate's other games (none from Ragdoll Rides or Mossbell Stand); the character lines' Sal Scorch and Vesper already exist, so the training villain is Gale and the illusionist Morrow.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest server fixes): a finale villain that has left EnemySim but whose Killed has not fired yet (it goes at the 10 Hz event drain) is "dying" (EnemySim.dying, EnemyService.dying), and SideQuestService waits for it instead of respawning a full-HP villain; only a villain gone without a defeat comes back. Fights count robots alive or dying the same way.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest server fixes): an escort's drive comes from IncidentSpots.drivePath: the farm road when it has a leg, else the longest of a few street paths from the site and street spots within 80 studs; a path still short is driven back along itself to the goal's length, and the need is never more than the drive. Old way: about 4% of escort builds jammed.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest server fixes): a pocket run starts only on a fresh step into its ring (SideQuestPlan.ringEntries), so a hero put back at the ring after a failed run or Leave is not pulled straight in; a refused try is tried again.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest server fixes): when a member leaves (or abandons, or switches slot) and the sides still there have all finished, the step completes (SideQuestPlan.leave's third result). A slot switch (DataService.Loaded) or a side change leaves the group, so a character never keeps another slot's side.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest server fixes): photo steps keep one spare Sneak Bot over the shots still needed (a beaten one is replaced); fight, chase and photo steps put out robots they could not build (the 90-robot cap) on later ticks; a fight built with none keeps its planned kill count. A robot type nate switches off (Config.Enemies `on`) gives way to the first type that is on (same medium, same AI first: SideQuestPlan.robotFor) for squads, runners, Sneak Bots and finale villains (a stand-in villain wears no dress).
- 2026-11-18 (story fixes): a mission run moves to its next stage only when it has tracks and all are done, never while a task force's comic panel is up (run.panel; Shared.MissionRules.advance). Between legs, and during the 0.6 s fade in, the run has no tracks: the old check read that as done, so a task force ran every leg and paid each one in about half a second, and every mission skipped its stage 1 during the fade in.
- 2026-11-18 (story fixes): on a full server (the 90-robot cap) a mission's boss, chase or defeat step waits and tries its robots again each tick: a boss or a chase never skips. A defeat step fights the robots that came (need = how many). A switched-off type lets the story move on, as fakes and twins already do. Kept inside MissionService: EnemySim's caps are unchanged.
- 2026-11-18 (story fixes): the last task force member to leave goes home through finishRun (still a member) before the pocket is freed. Squadmates pulled into a mission must be loaded, standing (not Downed), out of combat and not traveling (MissionRules.canJoin). The sidekick and mentor pay counts only members still in the run.
- 2026-11-18 (story fixes): the prologue marks a camera beat seen only when it moves off that beat (the ceremony was marked as it started, so first-timers saw SKIP). Its 330 s timeout restarts at PLAY THE PROLOGUE on a returning account's main menu. The hero's health floor is on from the first beat to the end (city robots can still target a hero in a scene, and the plaza had no floor).
- 2026-11-18 (story fixes): ProfileSchema.load keeps a newer save's version (a rollback) instead of writing the current one on it, so the newer build never runs its migrations twice. DataService still saves that session (repair keeps what it knows); refusing to save a newer save would need a DataService change.
- 2026-11-17 (art pass, side quests): every side quest giver, named helper and finale villain gets its own outfit (classic Shirt and Pants side by side, Config.NpcLooks.people, drawn by art/clothes/people.py) and, where the six shared faces don't fit, a face of its own. The outfit draws the suit, shirt, pants and legs slots (PeopleLooks.strip); hats, coats, gloves, boots, hair and capes stay from the creator look. At 0 the person keeps today's look exactly.
- 2026-11-17 (art pass, side quests): Lodestar (Last Partner) and Patch's relay keep their story looks (Lodestar is the prologue's hero figure, Patch is a radio): no outfit rows. Generic witnesses and carried citizens stay generic; only the seven named helpers (Wren, Toby, Flint, Finn, the Sunken City diver, Ambrose, Hoshi) stand in for their step (Config.NpcLooks.helpers).
- 2026-11-17 (art pass, side quests): the creature and machine finales get Blender models (art/blender/finales.py): the Bramblebeast (pumpkin head, bramble crown, bark and vines, a seed of light), the Voidhound (a hound-helmed humanoid machine, not a four-legged dog, so every fight animation still fits) and the Wind-Up Giant (a clockwork tin soldier). A finale's model wins over its outfit when both are present; without the import the game draws today's look.
- 2026-11-17 (art pass, side quests): the Tidewright gets a machine face (bronze plates, rivets, a grille), since it is a guardian machine; Vesper gets a cracked face of her own for her unmasking.
- 2026-11-17 (art pass, upload): make_package.py packages only the images still at 0 in the configs (--all for everything), adds the npc_person target, and the Studio resolver is now a template upload.ps1 fills from ids.json. apply_ids.py no longer rewrites the city materials from a batch with no city maps (it used to wipe all 22) and merges a partial city batch into the maps already set.
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): boss tiers are strings ("mvp" .. "floor"); PassService reads them with BossMath.tookPart (it compared one with 1, which threw out of the boss's reward loop: nobody after the first hero was paid, no result phase, no boss again on that server). BossService.finish pays each hero under its own pcall, so one failing reward or listener never stops the others or the fight's end.
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): a boss retreat pays its 25% Credits consolation and no Fame or Infamy (BossMath.reward's fame flag), as the M6 line says ("25% of the tier's Credits only"); heroes who left the server are dropped from the ledger before tiers (BossMath.present), so they hold no MVP or Contributor slot; PvP in the result phase pays in full (BossService.inFight is false once the fight is over).
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): a beaten robot whose Killed has not fired yet is not gone (EnemySim.gone = not alive and not dying): the Bounty no longer respawns its elite in that gap (the kill was lost about half the time) and Your double no longer fails when the twin was beaten.
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): Robot rampage's goal drops by the robots the server cap kept out of each wave (EventPlan.shortNeed, never below 1); an incident fight, find or leader step that got no robots at all tries its squad again every 3 s (Config.Incidents.capRetry) instead of waiting for the deadline.
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): a PvP defeat clears the loser's last attacker, so leaving inside the 15 s window no longer pays the winner twice. A new character always clears the Downed attribute.
- 2026-11-18 (open-world fixes): a Roblox Reset (PvpRules.resetOutcome): while Downed it is Give Up with Give Up's costs only (nothing past the defeat, rule 6); standing, within 15 s of PvP damage, it is that PvP defeat, the same as leaving then, so a Reset never dodges a lost fight; otherwise the plain defeat as before.
- 2026-11-18 (nate, via the decisions thread): keep the Reset rule as built: Reset while Downed = Give Up (no extra cost); Reset within 15 s of PvP damage = that PvP defeat (like leaving); otherwise a plain respawn (PvpRules.resetOutcome).
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes): ProcessReceipt answers PurchaseGranted only after a save that holds the receipt id worked (Shared.ShopRules.receipt): a receipt id already on the live profile is saved again first, a session that cannot save grants nothing, and a receipt that arrives during the profile load waits for it. A session that cannot save never opens a Robux prompt (reason "save").
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes): the Hero Pass is judged and granted on the live season (ShopRules.livePass rolls the profile first), so a pass bought at a season boundary is never set on the ended season.
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes): leaving runs in one order whatever order Roblox runs PlayerRemoving handlers in: DataService's Leaving, the beforeSave hooks (they read the live hero), then DataService.Left, where HeroService removes the hero (HeroService keeps a hero DataService still holds; a 10 s safety net). The hooks also run in a session that cannot save, so a slot switch keeps the old hero's live state.
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes, rule 6): one kick event is one kick: GuardRules clears the strike book at the kick and counts nothing while it runs (and GuardService has a kicking flag), so a first offender is never counted twice or queued for review. A speed strike needs fresh evidence: for Config.Safety.strikeGrace (3 s) after one, fails only pull back (one lag spike's stale positions used to stack 3 strikes in 0.2 s).
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes): Legacy keeps origin, mobility, carried weapon, the free path change once used, the side quest chains and the whole MMO record (nemesis, banes, trophies, mentor, worn title, aura, trim, travel styles, task force weeks); contracts, respec and Second Wind timers start over with the run.
- 2026-11-18 (data fixes): Signal.Fire runs each handler under xpcall (still in order and synchronous); TravelStyleService adds one Mobility listener a player and shows the new hero's style on a slot switch; Telemetry's shop funnel steps take an analytics budget slot.
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): a device works out the city kit's street props without the rest of the world (WorldMap.propsJob/propsStep: each district, then the outskirts tiles, keeping only the props), 4 tiles a step under a 2 ms slice of each frame (CityProps). Same list as WorldMap.build's, in the same order (tests, 3 seeds). Before: the whole WorldMap.build at once, 77 ms and +25 MB on a PC (a few hundred ms on a phone).
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): a dressed rig's 66 clothes Textures are drawn by distance per preset (Config.Presets clothesNear 20/25/30, clothesFar 60/80/100 for low/medium/high): all inside clothesNear, all but the 21 faces the body hides at rest out to clothesFar (Shared.ClothesMap.tucked: hips, soles, palms' ends, the inner sides of legs and arms, the torso's sides), none past it (the part colours match). Textures are unparented and put back, never remade (NpcRig.showClothes), with a 10% hold at each edge.
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): Enemy.Delta sends each player what its device draws (Shared.QualityRules.enemiesFor): the saved Graphics preset when fixed, else the highest the device class reaches on Auto (phone low 20, tablet medium 40, PC and console high 60; no hello yet high), plus Config.Net.enemySpare (4) so the camera's nearest are there. A packet never holds more than EnemyBuffer.MAX (65, 855 B: 900 less 40 for the remote). Analytics.device exposes the Telemetry hello's class.
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): EnemyBuffer.encodeFor keeps its scratch arrays and sorts the distances as plain numbers (a comparator sort only when two robots tie), byte for byte the old packets (tested, ties too); the robot renderer reuses its frame tables, sets the health bar only when health moves, poses robots per preset (Config.Presets enemyPoseNear/enemyPoseEvery: low 60 studs, every 4th frame; medium and high as before) and trims its body pool (enemyPool: 4 spares a kind, 2 on low, the rest destroyed after 30 s, 20 s on low, idle).
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): FrameProf.wrap makes no closure per call; BlockService's Heartbeat is under FrameProf; SideQuestService sends a member's tracker state only when it changes (the device already counts `left` down, so a timer is resent only when it drifts over a second); HeroService.simHeroes keeps one table a hero or decoy and writes it in place.
- 2026-11-18 (perf fixes): face decals are exported at 256 px (art/clothes/faces.py PX; people.py uses it), not 1024: a face sits on a 1-stud head. The pending upload package's 17 PersonFace images were replaced with the 256 px ones (the manifest and every other file unchanged); the 18 shared faces already uploaded at 1024 stay. FACE_PX=1024 redraws big ones for a close-up store render only.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily, gap G1): the daily login streak and daily quests are the account's (profile.daily, save v13; the day's contract count stays in the same block), on the UTC day (00:00 UTC, the daily contracts' reset). Rules in Shared.DailyRules, numbers in Config.Daily, paid by DailyService, drawn by DailyHud. The spec names the feature only (Update 1), so every number below is Claude's starting guess for nate to tune.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily, rule 6): a day's login counts the moment the profile loads (or at 00:00 UTC while on); its reward waits for CLAIM, and a reward still unclaimed at the next day's login is paid then, never lost. One missed day is forgiven once every 7 days (Config.Daily.grace); a longer gap starts the track over at day 1, keeping every title and the best streak.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily): once a day per account: the visit stamps the day and CLAIM clears `owed` before anything is paid, in the same profile as the wallet, so a rejoin or a server hop (the session lock hands the saved profile on) never pays twice; a server whose clock is behind never counts a day again; a session that cannot save (DataStore down) counts and pays nothing and shows the error state.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily, NATE TO TUNE): the 7-day track (it loops): day 1 300 Credits, day 2 20 Dust, day 3 one contract's Renown at your level + 200 Credits, day 4 500 Credits, day 5 40 Dust, day 6 two contracts' Renown + 300 Credits, day 7 a daily title + 800 Credits + 60 Dust (80 Dust instead once all 4 titles are owned). Credits and Dust sit at the Hero Pass's tier sizes, Renown at the contract scale (Config.Economy.earn.contract x Economy.scale). Paid flat (no friend, Legacy or 2x pass bonus).
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily, NATE TO TUNE): day 7 titles, one per finished week of the streak (draft names, side-neutral): Seven Days Strong, Two Weeks Strong, Round the Clock, Never Off Duty; title ids Config.Daily.titleBase (5000) + n in the H7 title list (Shared.BadgeRules.titles reads profile.daily.titles). Earnable only, no stat.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily, NATE TO TUNE): 3 daily quests a day, the same pool order for everyone that UTC day (seed: the day and Config.Daily.seedSalt), each player taking the first ones open at their level, no two on one key: clear 2 or 4 incidents, rescue 5 or 10 citizens, defeat 20 or 50 robots, finish 1 or 3 contracts, hit a world boss, finish a side quest step, help at a city event, throw 3 things (level gates 1 to 15). Each pays 0.6 to 1.6 contracts' worth of Credits and Renown at your level; all 3 add 1 contract's worth and 30 Dust.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily): PvP daily quests are off (Config.Daily.pvp = false, nate's call); when on, a PvP quest also counts a non-PvP way ("Win 2 PvP fights (or clear 2 incidents)"), so nobody is pushed into PvP.
- 2026-11-18 (WP1 daily): the claim card opens on join once the hero is free (made, the prologue done, no story scene), 4 s later. The small tracker is a DAILY n/3 pill under the top row that opens its 3 rows for 6 s when a quest moves; on a phone there is no spot clear of the corner group, the move stick and the action buttons, so it folds into the DAILY button's "DAILY n/3". New juice rows dailyClaim and dailyQuest; analytics events daily_login, daily_claim, daily_quest, daily_all_done.
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes, rule 6): MoveGuard's speed cap is MoveMath.guardCap: the style cap, a ride's speed and Weightless flight, times the class run bonus and 1 + the "speed" buff (Weightless, Feather, a Mystic's +25% on allies, Tide Mirror, Glide Step). Slows never lower it. Every pick's top speed, every ride and dash, PvP knocks, Meteor Leap and the Grappling Hook are tested against the budget.
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes, rule 6): MoveCheck keeps grants in their own pool, spent first and gone Config.Safety.grantKeep seconds after the last grant (now 2.0 s, was an unused 1.0), so old dashes never bank a later jump. An expired grant can only pull back, never strike. A fail that is no strike waits one sample before it pulls back, because a dash's positions can reach the server before its CombatUse grant. Teleports still strike on the first sample.
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes): leaps (Meteor Leap, Phoenix Dive) use the client's path on the server: a unit ray along the aim (never steeper than -0.2) finds the distance, and the hero travels it along the flat aim, landing on the floor below. A leap aimed 30 degrees up used to land 13 studs short of where the hero did. Dashes, slams and launches already matched.
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes): field ticks carry the row's slot and set (CastPlan.tickEff), so super fields keep PvP's 60% rule and the Epic set bonus. Awakening's damage in PvP is the pvpDamage 0.10 (rollDamage passes pvp). Team-up combos never pair two heroes who can hit each other (FlagService.canHit).
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes): server cooldowns for skills and gadgets start from max(now, readyAt) (Cooldowns.startNext), so the 0.1 s leeway lets a use in early but never shortens the cycle. Spamming a 0.5 s skill gave 24 casts in 10 s; it now gives at most 21. The charge cap no longer adds ping on top of the hold the server timed (press and release cross the same network); the 0.15 s leeway covers jitter.
- 2026-11-18 (combat fixes): CombatService.use refuses casts during fast travel (hero.traveling) and in the creator scene. Missions, HQ and the prologue are scenes with fights, so they still cast. A hero who leaves takes their fields, Soul Chains links, raft and pound timers along, and ended links and rafts are swept every 5 s. At the 40-field cap, the caster's own oldest field goes first.
- 2026-11-18 (UI fixes): Photo Mode moves from K to N (PhotoMode.KEY): K was also the Task Force board (InputController) and its safehouse prompt; the board keeps K as Tasks.md H4 says. N sits away from the combat keys, so a stray press mid-fight never freezes the hero. The controller hints follow H1's map (Skill 3 ◀, Skill 4 ▼, Block LB; POWERS has no pad button).
- 2026-11-18 (UI fixes): one main panel at a time (Kit.opening): POWERS, GEAR, MAP, JOURNAL, the Weapon Rack, the contract and task force boards, WANTED, MENTOR, BADGES, the Hero Pass and the HQ stations close each other as one opens; the shop and settings are overlays that open on top and close nothing. On a gamepad an opened panel selects its first button (GuiService.SelectedObject).
- 2026-11-18 (UI fixes): the Downed and Defeated screen has its own ScreenGui at DisplayOrder 25 (over the shop 18, the creator 20 and settings 22) and going Downed closes every Kit panel; Photo Mode refuses to open during a cutscene or while Downed, and either closes it.
- 2026-11-18 (UI fixes): a pending button's wait is tagged (Kit.pending), so an earlier tap's 5 s timer never shows TRY AGAIN or unlocks a later tap; the plate menu's report reasons replace its buttons inside the menu; the news screens' SurfaceGuis (in PlayerGui) turn ResetOnSpawn off, as every client gui in PlayerGui now does (a test checks the source).
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards): three boards, the spec's: Boss MVPs (a world boss beaten as MVP), Rescues (IncidentService.Rescued: incidents, events and contracts; plus the Sunken Line's commuters through EconomyService.Earned "deepRescue") and PvP Wins (a new DeathService.PvpWon, fired only for a win that paid: two flagged heroes, past the anti-farming ledger, so traded or repeat wins never count). CitizenService has no rescues of its own (ambient citizens only), so nothing hooks there. No other spec board fit (the spec lists these three).
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards): one OrderedDataStore a week named Weekly_<week> (the spec's name; PassRules.week, Monday 00:00 UTC), one scope per board, key the user id, value the week's total. The total lives on the save in profile.mmo.counts ("board.week", "board.<id>", "board.pending", "board.pmask", "board.titles"), so each write is a SetAsync of the total: a retry never counts twice and a rolled-back save rolls the board back with it. Kept in mmo.counts (repair already keeps its string keys) so ProfileSchema stays untouched while WP1 bumps it; move to a profile.boards block in a later save version if nate likes.
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards, spec number): the spec's "Batched every 5 minutes per server" is built as a 5 s write pass with at most one write per stat per player per 60 s (Config.Boards.writeEvery), never more than 60% of Roblox's sorted-write budget (30 + 5 a player) in any minute; a leave and shutdown flush at once (only Roblox's 6 s key floor). 30 busy players for 10 minutes stay at most 90 writes a minute (tests). writeEvery = 300 gives the spec's 5 minutes exactly.
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards): reads are the top 100 once a minute per board per server; the screen shows the top 50, FRIENDS is your Roblox friends found in that top 100 (no extra reads, as Ragdoll Rides), your own rank past 100 shows "100+". Equal totals share a rank (1, 2, 2, 4) and show by user id.
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards): names on the boards are Roblox display names (Roblox filters them; Plates already show them under the hero name, Decision 106), as Ragdoll Rides did; never user names or typed text. The hero's title + noun name is not used because the board is per account (a store value is one number) and an offline player's hero name is not readable.
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards, NEEDS NATE OK): the weekly top-10 reward is a title per board, earned only, never sold, never a stat: Boss Breaker, City Saver, Crater Champion (Config.Boards titles, ids 6001 to 6003, above the daily titles at 5000 in the H7 title list). No existing earnable title or aura fit without cheapening another (the Fame titles belong to Fame ranks, the accolade auras to badge groups), so these are new names in the existing title system. Granted once: the week a player counted in waits on the save until 15 minutes after it ends, then the next join (or the next minute online) reads the final ranks once; a shared 10th place earns it.
- 2026-11-18 (WP2 weekly boards): the plaza board stands on the plaza's west edge (tower centre -44, +20), south of the Safehouse stairwell, facing the tower; the model streams to every device (Persistent) and each device draws one SurfaceGui (ResetOnSpawn off) that redraws only when the server's top-10 digest changes.
- 2026-11-18: tests/run.sh wraps the Lune suite (one run at a time via flock, 300 s watchdog, 4 tries). Lune 0.8.9 can freeze for good when several runs share the CPUs; alone the full suite takes under a minute.
- 2026-11-18 (WP6 robot combat, gap G9): the Warden (kind NullSentinel) braces on a timer as the spec's "Null Sentinels block on a timer" (Config.Guard.robotBlocks.NullSentinel, NATE TO TUNE: every 9 s for 2.5 s, first after 3 s, the front 240 degrees, 85% off, 6 s with no brace after a shatter). While braced its block stands in for its 120-degree front guard (CombatService hits it without the position, so no double cut and no flank break inside the 240); a hit from behind still breaks the guard. A breaker shatters the guard and the brace together; a broken guard never braces.
- 2026-11-18 (WP6 robot combat, gap G9): Bulwark's battle form, the War-Frame (BossSim NullWarFrame), blocks on a timer with a telegraph: 6 s into the fight and every 14 s, a 1.2 s blue build-up (the shine fades in over a filling blue ground ring, a rising hum; hits land in full), then 3.5 s of a block on its front 200 degrees (75% off; the back weak point stays open), never during its phase shield. A breaker (heavy, full charge, heavy throw) shatters it: SHATTER!, no block for 10 s, and it reels: its next attack waits 1 s (attacks already warned still land). Its own attacks stay boss attacks (dodged, not interrupted). Lives in BlockService (watchBoss from MissionService), not BossSim. NATE TO TUNE.
- 2026-11-18 (WP6 robot combat, gap G9): GuardMath robot blocks take an optional `warn` (a telegraph before the block, "warn" then "start"; busy or out of the fight calls it off) and `size` (a boss's shine, drawn where the event says). The Brute Bot's block is unchanged.
- 2026-11-18 (WP6 robot combat, gap G9): the Sneak Bot's lunge (Config.Enemies.SneakBot.lunge, NATE TO TUNE): 8 to 18 studs off, once every 7 s, it stops, shows itself (the cloak drops) and coils for 0.65 s with red eyes (the windup flag) while a red lane fills along the ground to a spot 3 studs past the hero (CombatEvents id 17, CombatFx.lane, a rising whine); then it dashes 0.45 s at 60 studs a second and strikes the hero still in the lane once at 1.4, a breaker against a block (the hit carries `breaker`). Any interrupt in the wind-up (a light hit, a stun, a knock, through BlockService as for every robot wind-up) cuts it off with CUT OFF!; stepping out of the lane dodges it. Evil twins on the sneak brain have no lunge. No new flag bits: it rides the windup bit and EXTRA.lunge.
- 2026-11-18 (WP6 robot combat, gap G10): downed robots are throwables: a robot a hero beats leaves a wreck where it fell (ThrowService on EnemyService.Killed), kind "wreck", medium (1.6, knock, a small blast on walls), anyone lifts it, the usual carry rules. It lies 10 s, or 3 s once thrown or set down, flickers its last 2 s, then pops apart in sparks; at most 6 at once on a server (a new one clears the oldest nobody holds; if all are held, no new one). Fakes, twins, costumed villains, creatures, sea robots and the small pack robots (Swarmers, Wind-Up Toys) leave none, and nor does a kill with no hero behind it. Sparks and chips come from the device's debris budget. NATE TO TUNE.
- 2026-11-18 (WP4 funnel): the gap check's missing Hero MMO funnel steps are a 6th funnel, MMO (first_block, first_throw, first_nemesis, first_taskforce, first_badge, pass_bought; Roblox allows 10). It is a lifetime funnel (FunnelDef.lifetime, FunnelKit.LIFE_SESSION): each step logs once per account in any order, kept as one bit each in profile.analytics.mmo (no save version bump; repair adds 0). A step is marked only after the per-minute budget lets it through, so a full budget or a loading profile retries it every 5 s (AnalyticsHooks) and never loses it. The budget rule moved to Shared.AnalyticsSchema.take so Lune tests it. PurchaseService gains a Landed signal (key, kind) for pass_bought.
- 2026-11-18 (WP4 favorite): after a hero's first world boss win the server asks the client once per account (profile.analytics.fav) to show Roblox's own favorite prompt: AvatarEditorService:PromptSetFavorite(game.PlaceId, Enum.AvatarItemType.Asset, true), in a pcall. It waits 8 s and for a calm moment (no cutscene, not Downed, no hit for 5 s), gives up after 180 s, and is a plain ask: no reward, no repeat, nothing changes either way (Roblox rules). The flag saves when the client reports the prompt shown, so a hero who leaves before it shows is asked after the next boss win.
- 2026-11-18 (WP4 rematch): after a PvP defeat the Defeated screen shows a fixed REMATCH button for 45 s (Config.Pvp.rematch); it sends Pvp.Rematch (rate-limited, no text) and the winner sees a fixed "NAME WANTS A REMATCH" card with ACCEPT and NO for 15 s. One ask per defeat, 120 s between asks for the same pair, refused when either player is unflagged or gone, and silently declined when the winner muted the loser. Accepting only pings each player's spot to the other (10 s): no teleport, no forced fight, nothing paid (Shared.RematchRules, FlagService).
- 2026-11-18 (WP5 Suspicious Hero): incident 25, SuspiciousHero (level 6, street, both sides): a crowd of 7 with one fake hero dressed as a generic costumed hero (Gleamheart, Steelvow or Quickstep in Config.Incidents.dress, never the famous three or lore names) at half health; its face cracks at the existing 50% (Config.Enemies.fakes.crack). Vigil heroes then scrap it before it slips away (the same fake, a step row with keep = true); Syndicate heroes hold the square for 8 s while the panic spreads. Steps may now have one row per side (IncidentPlan.rows); FakeRules.dress falls back to Config.Incidents.dress after Config.SideQuests.dress.
- 2026-11-18 (WP5 news and badge): IncidentService.FakeUnmasked feeds NewsService (two new news kinds, fakeVigil and fakeSyndicate, appended as codes 17 and 18) and BadgeService (new counter "fakes" and the Fake Spotter badge, Unmask 10 fake heroes, added at the end of the defeat group so no bit moves). Lune counts change: 25 templates, 19 for heroes, 22 for villains, 12 step kinds, 19 at level 50.
- 2026-11-18 (WP3 Rogues File, gap G3): the Journal's ROGUES tab holds 45 pages in book order: 20 robot kinds, 4 famous fakes (the evil twin included), 18 side quest finale villains (one per chain, plus the Gala Heist's and the Harvest King's encores; the Toymaker's page is his Wind-Up Giant fight) and the 3 world bosses. Built from Config.Rogues (fixed bits and the one-line blurbs) over Config.Enemies, the chains' finale name cards and dresses, Config.Boss and the bosses' name cards (Shared.RoguesFile). A page not beaten yet is a black silhouette with "???" and a hint where to meet it.
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): page bits in the account's 128-bit profile.rogues, never moved: 1-7 world bosses (Config.Boss `rogues` 1 to 3 kept, as BossService has set since M6), 9-15 their gold pages, 16-47 robots, 48-63 fakes, 64-119 finale villains, 120-127 the milestones paid. A test checks unique bits, every Config.Enemies kind, live chain finale and boss covered, all inside the save's words.
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): pages fill server side through signals (RoguesService): EnemyService.Killed fills the robot or fake page for the hero who landed the defeat (the same credit as the kill badges); a robot in a finale dress never fills its suit's robot page; a finished chain (SideQuestService.StepPaid, which now also passes the chain id) fills its finale villains for every member paid; a beaten world boss (BossService.Rewarded, which now also passes the fight's level) fills its page at any tier.
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): a gold page is a world boss beaten in a fight at level 40 or higher (the Heroic band's floor, Config.Rogues.gold.level) as a Contributor or the MVP. One gold page per boss (3).
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): each page keeps its first day (UTC), the level it was first beaten at and how many times (capped at 65,535, shown 999+) in profile.roguesLog[bit as text]. The field is additive (repair fills and cleans it) and needs no save version bump, so it merges with wp1-daily's bump.
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): collection rewards, once per account: 10 pages the Antenna Helm, 25 pages the Circuit Suit, every page the Ember Aura. All three are existing creator "drop" pieces (earnable from Epic and Legendary drops too), never sold, looks only. A milestone is paid even when the piece is already owned (its bit marks it), with the 10 PAGES! stamp.
- 2026-11-18 (WP7 Journal tabs, gap G4): the Journal has 8 tabs in two rows of four left of X (STORY, QUESTS, REP, PERKS / ROGUES, BADGES, MENTOR, SHOWDOWN); the JOURNAL title made way for the second row. BADGES shows BadgeHud's Badge Book, Collections and Titles views (a three-button row on top), MENTOR the mentor card, SHOWDOWN the WANTED poster with SHOWDOWN, which starts the fight and closes the Journal. Each Hud exports drawInto, request and a Changed signal; the WANTED, MENTOR and BADGES screens stay as they are, and the Journal stays one main panel (Kit.opening).
- 2026-11-18 (WP3): a dressed robot or fake (any EnemyService.identity with dress = true: the side quest finale villains, and the Suspicious Hero costumes in Config.Incidents.dress on WP5) never fills its suit kind's page; the finale villains get their own pages on the finale, a Suspicious Hero costume gets none.
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 group and codes, gap G12): both ship OFF until nate sets them (Config.Promo): group.id 0 hides the group row, an empty codes list hides the code box. No other code changes when nate fills them in (Launch Checklist "Group and creator codes").
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 group, NEEDS NATE OK): the group-join reward is the Spark Badge (an existing suit emblem from the "drop" pool: earnable from Epic and Legendary drops, never sold, looks only), once per account, and it stays yours if you leave the group (rule 6). The server checks Player:IsInGroup in a pcall when the save loads and when CHECK is tapped (CHECK also asks GroupService:GetGroupsAsync, since IsInGroup can keep an earlier answer for the session), at most once per 20 s per player; a failed Roblox call pays nothing and says "Could not reach Roblox", never "not a member".
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 codes): creator codes are a FIXED list in Config.Promo.codes ({ code, piece, starts?, ends?, tag? }); a typed code only ever matches a row exactly (no prefixes, no near misses, no player-made codes). Codes are 3 to 12 of A to Z and 0 to 9; the box upper-cases and drops anything else as you type, and the server also forgives spaces, dashes and underscores. starts and ends are UTC dates, both days included.
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 codes): each code pays once per account, forever: profile.promo.codes[CODE] = the UTC day, kept even after the row is removed, so putting a code back never pays twice. A code whose piece you already own still counts as used ("You already own the X"); no substitute reward, so codes never stack value. Rewards must be "drop" creator pieces (PromoRules.validate, tested): never a sold, pass, starter, level, rank, mission or boss piece, never a stat.
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 no free text): the code box is on the typer's own Settings screen only. PromoRedeem is one string of at most 20 characters (0.5 a second, burst 3), plus a wait after 5 wrong codes in 60 s (Config.Promo.tries; only unknown or malformed codes count). The server never stores, logs, echoes or broadcasts the typed text: the answer is a fixed reason (PromoResult), shown as one fixed line under the box, plus the usual NEW LOOK PIECE toast. The line sits in the panel because the Toasts layer (DisplayOrder 8) is under Settings (22).
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 save and analytics): profile.promo = { group = 0/1, codes = {} } is additive with no save version bump (repair fills and cleans it: real code keys only, at most 200) and stays server-side (not in the client view). One new event, promo_claim (kind group or code, the row's tag or its code from the fixed list), once per claim, so it sits well inside the per-minute budget.
- 2026-11-18 (WP10 UI): Settings gets a REWARDS block at the bottom (under MUTED PLAYERS): "JOIN THE GROUP: FREE SPARK BADGE" with CHECK / CLAIMED, and the code box with REDEEM; each shows only while it is on (the server's PromoState says so; it counts live codes, never lists them). Studio-only codes STUDIOTEST (live) and STUDIOOLD (ended) and Dev panel buttons (forget claims, group member) are for the Studio list; they never work on a live server.
- 2026-11-18 (WP11 story acts, gap G14): the story holds acts: Config.Story.acts lists them in play order (Act 1 out; Acts 2 and 3 `soon`), and every mission and choice names its act (all of today's are Act 1). Mission numbers stay global (the save's first-clear bits), so a later act's missions take the numbers after Act 1's 6 and its choices the save slots after 3. Adding an act is data only: its missions, choices and ending, then drop `soon` (StoryPlan.validate checks the data).
- 2026-11-18 (WP11): StoryPlan's gates read the act: a mission needs the one played before it (the last of the act before for an act's first), a coming-soon act's missions are always locked, never next, never pinned and their choices never pick; the pin reads "Mission 3: Framed" in Act 1 as before and "Act 2, Mission 1: <name>" in a later act (missions count from 1 within their act). actSkillPoints takes an act (Act 1 still 8).
- 2026-11-18 (WP11): story.act (in the save since M7, never written until now) is StoryPlan.currentAct: the first act not done, else the last one out, so it stays 1 while Act 2 is coming soon; StoryService saves it and sends it with StoryState. The Progress funnel's "Act 1 done" stage read story.act >= 2 and so could never fire; it now also reads Act 1's clears.
- 2026-11-18 (WP11): save room, additive with no version bump (the shape of an Act 1 save is unchanged): mission bits repair to Config.Story.saveRoom.missions (24, was 6), choice slots to saveRoom.choices (12, was 3; one slot per Config.Story.choices entry by default, still 3), story.act to the acts listed. A newer build's later-act clears, picks and act survive a load (the rollback rule of 2026-11-18).
- 2026-11-18 (WP11): "Act 1 done" means Act 1's own missions (StoryPlan.actDone(Config, story, 1)), not "the last mission in the list", in HqRules (the act1 HQ trophy), BadgeService, TaskForceService, MissionService's act_done funnel step, RepTiers.act1 (the rep pace after Act 1) and the Dev panel's "Act 1 done", so a later act's missions never move them.
- 2026-11-18 (WP11): the Journal's STORY tab gets an act row on top (ACT 1, ACT 2, ACT 3: gold the one shown, paper an act that is out, grey a coming-soon one), the same small-row pattern as the BADGES tab's views, so the two rows of main tabs and the phone layout do not move. It opens on the hero's act. An act that is out lists its missions, its own choices, the PIN row and a locked row per act still to come; a coming-soon act's page is only "Act N: coming soon" with LOCKED. Config.Story.actText holds the only words a later act shows.
- 2026-11-18 (WP11, lore rule): no Act 2 or Act 3 beats, names, villains or plot are written anywhere in code or config; their story is nate's to decide.

Nothing imported: every box shows as today (the fallback).

- 2026-11-18 (set piece art, what stays parts): the text signs (THE MUSEUM GALA, THE RACERS' MEET), the gala's red carpet, the meet's chequered line and every pocket's floors, walls, stairs and water stay parts; no look ever hides a sign. SetPieceKit.check (tests) keeps every look over the boxes it hides (their middle on the meshes, the meshes at most 6 studs past them). The script formats the fit table with the repo's stylua so a re-run writes the committed file.

- 2026-11-18 (vehicle art, escorts): SetPieceKit.TRUCKS gives every chain with an escort its own truck: HeroAcademy FieldTripBus (the Halo Academy's yellow bus with its halo sign), WindUpLane IceCreamTruck (Scoops's), SaltDogs FerryVan (the Gull Point ferry crew's), TheScoop NewsVan (Nora's; an orange band, not red, so it never reads as an ambulance, and no red and blue light bar), LastOwner ShowVan (Ambrose the Astonishing's), SuitRecall PartsTruck (Tinker Tess's); HarvestKing keeps setpieces.py's FarmTruck and the Dev panel's sample escort keeps the motorcade box. ThrowService now puts back each part's own CanQuery after a carry (it set every part queryable), so a thrown car's meshes still take no queries.
- 2026-11-18 (vehicle art, budget): at most 2,500 triangles an escort (one on screen per group), 1,000 a car and 1,500 a tram or truck; all 40 hub vehicles in their looks come to about 37,000 triangles over the whole hub (a street shows a few at a time).

## Proposals (NOT decided)
Drafts for nate only (WP11, 2026-11-18). None of these is in code or config; they are systems ideas, not lore, and no Act 2 or 3 story is proposed here.
- When an act comes out, a returning hero who finished Act 1 could get a one-time "Act 2 is here" toast and the Journal opening on the new act (currentAct already moves to it).
- The coming-soon row could later carry an optional teaser line nate writes (a `teaser` field on the act), shown only once he adds one.
- Act 2 could reuse the mission pocket system (Config.Story.pockets has 7; a new act's missions may need more pockets or reuse Act 1's layouts).
- A later act's story choices could read Act 1's flags (StoryPlan.flags already reads every choice), so Act 1 picks carry forward.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8 live tuning, gap G11): the spec's Balance_v<N> is one DataStore per bundled balance version, named "Balance_v" .. Config.Version.balance (Balance_v1 now), with one key "live" (Config.Balance). Bumping Config.Version.balance in a build moves the store, so an old live key never lands on top of new bundled numbers.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8): BalanceService.load runs at the top of the server boot, before any other service is required, so every module (even one that copies a number into a local) sees the tuned numbers. It waits at most Config.Balance.timeout (4 s, 2 tries); any trouble runs the bundled Config: the switch off, an unpublished place, Studio without API access, an error, no key, a bad value or no answer in time (a late answer is dropped, so numbers never change mid-game). The result prints at boot ("[Balance] v1.3 live ...") and the boot line shows the tag.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8): only a whitelist can change, each number inside its own range (Config.Balance.keys, Shared.BalanceMerge): the Credits and Renown scale, each Economy.earn source's base and per-level pay, each contract type's pay, the robots' shared level scaling, each robot kind's hp and hit coefficient (0.5x to 2x its own), the world boss's HP and its wait between bosses (shortest <= longest). "*" only ever matches keys that already exist; no new keys, no new array slots.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8): devices keep the bundled Config, so the whitelist holds only numbers the server alone reads (a test scans src/client for every one). Left out on purpose: drop odds and pity (shown in Loot Info), the level curve (it would move saved levels), the Heroic pay tag and the first-5 contract count (shown on devices), cooldowns, speeds and anything a device simulates; and never ids, bits, orders, prices, the 2x pass or anything sold (locked sections Version, Balance, Data, Net, Places, Safety, Shop, Pass, Creator, Weapons, Hq, Analytics, Loot, Settings and locked words id, num, typeId, bit, rogues, order, price, robux, product, cost are refused even if listed). Pushing the same numbers to devices is a later step if nate wants device-side numbers live.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8): a refused number never stops the good ones; each refusal has a reason (unknown, locked, type, nan, inf, range, int, duplicate, order) and a key with over 200 numbers or 8 levels deep is refused whole. Out-of-range numbers are refused, not clamped, so a typo (40 for 4.0) never lands as the cap. "_rev" (a whole number) is the key's revision for the logs; other "_" keys are notes.
- 2026-11-18 (WP8): analytics event balance_live once per player session (value: numbers applied; fields: "v<N>.<rev>", the source (live, none, bundled, off, unpublished, studio, error, timeout), numbers refused), so every KPI can be split by the balance a player played on. Not low priority; one per join is far inside the per-minute budget. Dev panel: "Balance: print" prints the whole result on the server.
- 2026-11-18 (WP9 WANTED poster, gap G5, spec H5): the HQ's WANTED poster and nemesis heads hang on the east wall between the pilasters at z 24 and 48, right of the Trophy Wall (Config.Hq.wanted), in both the Safehouse and the Foundry: the south wall's ends hold the tier dressing and the middle the Hall of Legends. The poster is a paper part with a SurfaceGui (the nemesis's bust in a ViewportFrame, name, rank stars, Intel, a note and a stamp); its B prompt opens the WANTED panel (NemesisHud), as does WANTED POSTER on the Trophy Wall panel.
- 2026-11-18 (WP9): the poster shows the same nemesis as the WANTED button (NemesisMath from the user id, rank and side): a black silhouette with "???" and a LEVEL 8 stamp below level 8, a silhouette with ON ITS WAY for the day after a Showdown win, SHOWDOWN READY at 3 Intel; the bust and heads are built from the same pieces NemesisFx puts on its Brute Bot body (colours, head piece, build, cape, trait pieces; Shared.WantedRules).
- 2026-11-18 (WP9): one head per nemesis rank beaten (5 mounts, rank 1 on the left), each the look of that rank's nemesis; past rank 5 a win adds no head (the spec's "its head for your HQ wall" per nemesis, and the ranks stop at 5). Heads are always shown and take no trophy slot. Read from the save as it is (mmo.nemesis.rank, mmo.heads, mmo.banes): rank r means 1 to r - 1 beaten, mmo.heads counts wins, and a bane seed is matched to its rank and side, so there is no new save field and no version bump (save stays v13).
- 2026-11-18 (WP9): Dev "nemesis 2" (Dev panel "Nemesis beaten") counts a Showdown win through the same ownerWin as a real Showdown, with the next nemesis ready at once, and redraws the HQ.
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, bug): a Damaged building's real body stops blocking server raycasts (setWrecked turns CanQuery off so shots pass its holes), so bolts, dashes, lines, leaps and throws aimed at it passed through every wall it still had and landed beyond it, unable to finish it off. CombatService's rayDist, line of sight and ground checks now also stop on its standing Structure pieces (BuildingState.ray through BuildingService.ray): only the holes let shots through.
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, bug): damaging fields (supers like Blizzard Dome, Singularity and Sunfall, and skills like Static Field and Ember Ring), damaging rides, launches and placed things broke nothing, against nate's "every damaging ability breaks buildings". Their cast now hits the buildings at its centre with the usual structure damage (super 3,000, skill 700; BuildingState.castStruct and abilityStruct, shared so a test walks every ability row). Parry ripostes stay out (they strike the attacker, not a place).
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, bug): a skyscraper's crown (setbacks, spire, lantern, mast; up to 312 studs above its shaft) took no hits, since a blast had to be within 10 studs of the shaft's roof. A blast up to its peak now touches the building and lands on the shaft's top storey (BuildingState.touches, blastY), so the roof and top walls break and the crown goes with them (BuildingFx already hides the crown once the top storey has a hole).
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, bug, spec change): "a 4th collapse is cut to Damaged" left a building whose ground storey had given way standing on nothing until someone hit it again. Such a building is now held and comes down by itself once the 6 s collapse window has room (BuildingState.stepHeld, every 0.5 s), longest held first; still at most 3 collapses at once.
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, bug): a building repaired and hit again inside one 0.5 s diff batch kept its old holes on every device (the state read Damaged before and after, so devices never cleared them). Repairs now go out first in each batch (BuildingDiff r); the device's copy is updated by the shared BuildingState.applyDiff (repairs, then pieces, then states) and applySnapshot (a snapshot replaces every hole), and a repair drops its pieces still waiting to be sent (the Dev panel's repair all).
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, perf): many storeys falling at once (the collapse cap counts only full collapses) could animate thousands of pieces a frame on every device in range. Each preset now caps the pieces animating in falls (Config.Presets falling: low 240, medium 600, high 1,000, so one whole building always falls in full); past the cap a piece just goes in the dust.
- 2026-11-18 (city audit, leak): BuildingFx kept the saved collision flags of a Damaged building's parts after it streamed out (new parts come back each time), and CityProps kept the old preset's props in cells it was building when the preset changed. unregister drops the flags; CityProps counts a preset generation and rebuilds a pass that changed under it.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: analytics budget): the per-minute budget is a sliding 60 s window of send times (Shared.AnalyticsSchema.take), not a minute that starts at its first send: the old window let a burst at the end of one minute and another at the start of the next send twice the cap (120 + 20 a player) inside one 60 s span (test: 639 sends for a cap of 320). Holds whatever window Roblox's own throttle uses.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: analytics budget): FunnelKit's sends (session_start, stage_reached, session_end, first_session_end, the Progress and Returns steps) go through AnalyticsSchema.force: never dropped, but counted inside the window. The old sink added to a stale minute's count, which the next send reset, so a join burst after a quiet spell was not counted at all; its funnel steps were not counted ever.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: analytics budget): every funnel step takes a budget slot (Combat, PvP, Story and Purchase steps and the Opening funnel took none; a 20% sample of casts alone opened a Combat funnel session about 2 times a second a fighting hero). A step the budget refuses waits and Analytics.flush sends it with the 5 s funnel checks. A refused event (unknown name, too many fields) no longer spends a slot.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: device facts): the Telemetry remote is paced per player and kind on the server (Config.Analytics.devicePace: perf_sample 50 s, shop_open 5 s, shop_view_item 1 s, photo event 2 s; Shared.AnalyticsSchema.pace), so one device can no longer spend the server's budget at the remote's 1 a second (30 devices could send 1,800 a minute). The Photo recon contract still counts every shot.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: funnel order): every funnel logs each step at most once and in order: a step reached before the step ahead of it waits (a reached bit) and goes out the moment the gap fills (Shared.AnalyticsSchema gates, AnalyticsSchema.KINDS). The Opening funnel kept only "the furthest step", so the prologue's reward (level 3, step 9) skipped first_incident (step 8) for every new player, for good.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: funnel kinds): Combat and Story are lifetime ladders (once per account, in order, on FunnelKit.LIFE_SESSION, the gate in profile.analytics.combat/combatReach and story/storyReach; additive, no save version bump). Each cast opened a new Combat funnel session, and act_done, mission replays and set bonuses on respawn counted again. PvP (one flagged stretch, opened by flag_on, closed by flag off) and Purchase (one shop visit, opened by shop_open) stay recurring, each step once a session; a step with no session open is not logged (a starter-card buy is a purchase event, not a shop funnel step).
- 2026-11-18 (audit, spec number change, NEEDS NATE OK): the PvP funnel is flag_on, first_pvp_hit, pvp_fight (a PvP defeat on either side), pvp_win (was flag_on, first_pvp_hit, pvp_win, pvp_loss, flag_off: a win and a loss are the two ends of one fight, so in order every loser skipped pvp_win and flag_off counted only after both). flag_off stays an event; a loss is the defeat event with cause pvp. first_pvp_hit now counts once per flagged stretch (the pvp_hit event stays once a server session). Not launched, so nothing on the dashboard renumbers.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: Opening funnel and D1): the Onboarding funnel's d1_return step now counts a day-1 return only once every step before it is logged (a funnel step, in order). Plain D1 retention is the Dashboard's Retention page, the Returns funnel or session_start's days_away "1d" (for the Soft Launch Plan's KPI table; the lead's doc).
- 2026-11-18 (audit: once per account): a session whose save did not load (DataService gave a blank profile that never saves) logs no funnel step, no FunnelKit session and no once-per-account event; only AnalyticsSchema.NOSAVE (session_device tagged "nosave", balance_live, perf_sample, idle_wait, anti_exploit_flag, report_player). Before, such a rejoin counted a returning player as new: session 1, the onboarding funnel, level ups from 1, badges and firsts again.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: fields): pvp_win's "paid" field reads DeathService.PvpWon (a win past the anti-farming ledger) and was always "1"; anti_exploit_flag's second field is named "result" (strike or kick, as sent; it said "strikes").
- 2026-11-18 (audit: field values): fields keep few distinct values (Roblox caps distinct custom field values per event; the exact rule to confirm, see Tasks): perf_sample's fps_p5 to multiples of 5 from 0 to 240 (a device could send any number to 1,000), level_up's second field is the session length bucket (session_len, FunnelKit.lengthBucket) instead of raw minutes in session (50 levels x hundreds of minutes), and session_end's activity says "Scene" without the scene id.
- 2026-11-18 (audit: no new events): no event was added. WP3's Rogues File has none (pages are saved but not logged), and WP5's Suspicious Hero logs through incident_done (template SuspiciousHero) and badge_earned (Fake Spotter). A Rogues page event is nate's call.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: fast travel): a fast travel sets the hero down where Shared.Travel.arrival says (beside the beacon post, 6 above the disc), shared by BeaconService and the test that checks every beacon's landing and every safehouse respawn point is clear of parts (all were; now it stays that way).
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: late teleports): MoveGuard.reset also tells MoveCheck to expect the hero at the new spot for Config.Safety.teleportKeep (6 s): a device whose lag outlasts the 1 s grace (it keeps sending the old spot, then the new one) had the late arrival read as a teleport, a strike and a pull back to where it came from; now that arrival is accepted and the expectation is spent (also spent when the move lands on time, so it is never a free jump back). Speed fails still only pull back.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: stale landings): a fast travel whose hero is moved by SafehouseService.place during the 3 s flight (a mission pocket, the HQ, a contest pad; hero.placedAt) no longer lands them back out at the beacon (Travel.landOk); FastTravelDone still goes so the camera comes back. MoveGuard.reset makes no track for a hero who already left.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: commuters): a hero who fast travels lets go of a Sunken Line commuter (they swim back to their air pocket), so the rescue is always swum to the quay, never flown there.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: citizens): CitizenSim.new takes the world's parts (CitizenSim.blocker over the hub boxes at citizen height): sidewalk corners inside a part and links through one are left out (the Clock Tower stood on Clock Square's inner corners, and two Old Town safehouse huts on a block side), each shelter gets a corner with a clear walk in, or a new door node on the nearest sidewalk link; a fleeing citizen with no sidewalk route to any shelter ducks into a doorway (hides at once) instead of walking straight through the blocks.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: vehicles): each vehicle keeps a room (Vehicles.place: its own street's kerbs, Old Town's narrow lanes, the Docks truck lane) and the flip's travel is cut at it, so a car pushed toward the kerb rolls over on the spot instead of landing in the building past the sidewalk (142 of 640 tested landings did); the client's arc is the same maths. A flipped vehicle's 30 s reset waits (2 s at a time) while a hero stands in its home spot (Vehicles.occupied).
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: sunken caches): a Trench cache the ring put inside the sunken factory's hall (cache 9, out of reach) moves out past the hall's nearest wall by the cache reach; the other nine caches and the commuters were clear and are now tested.
- 2026-11-18 (travel audit: sea robots): a sea robot moved out of the water from outside its own think (Lift and Throw's landing, a push) goes back to the last spot it was wet at on its next tick, so a Diver never chased heroes under the street; a Torpedo Sentry's torpedo pops at the surface (EnemySim.torpedoDone, on the server and in the device's drawing alike), so leaping out of the sea dodges it. Other sea shots are unchanged.
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 HQ visits, gap G15, spec Update 2): a visit is a read-only copy of the owner's HQ built from their save on the same server, never a teleport and never the owner's live cell: HqService.view builds the one HqState packet for any centre (the owner's own cell uses it too), and Shared.VisitRules.readOnly keeps the look (rooms, side, level, trophy wall picks, statues, props, reputation dressing, the WANTED poster and heads as the owner sees them, the dummies as decor) and drops everything that acts (upgrade, retire, mannequins, picks, drones, dummy ids, the boss sim). It redraws when the owner's HQ changes (HqService.ownerChanged, a slot switch).
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 pockets): each host (an owner with visitors) gets a visit pocket of its own on the HQ cells' grid (x 9,000+, z 9,000+, 260 apart, 25 a row) but at y 5,200, 700 studs over the HQ cells (y 4,500) and 2,200 over the mission pockets (y 3,000), off the 4,800 map: 60 pockets, the lowest free one taken (VisitRules.freeIndex), so slots never decide it. A test checks every pocket against every HQ cell (HeroService.MAX_SLOTS), every mission pocket and the city. The server builds the cell's floor and walls (HqService.buildCell), the device the rooms; the pocket comes down when its last visitor goes.
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 what visitors may do): only look and leave. Visitors are in the scene "hqVisit" (no PvP, robots, incidents, events, finds or drops); HqService's station remotes only answer the owner inside their own cell, the shop and creator refuse scenes, and the device draws the pads with "Owner only" and no prompts, the poster without its prompt and the dummies as decor no skill aims at; the exit pad and LEAVE HQ (gold, in the top row at order 0, and atop SQUAD) put them back exactly where they stood when the owner accepted (outside their own HQ's door if they were in it). Casting is allowed and hits nothing. No Lodestar in a copy (she is the owner's guide; one-line change in GuideClient).
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 asks, NEEDS NATE OK): anyone on the server can be asked (VISIT beside every player in SQUAD: squadmates, free players and players in other squads), across sides too (a villain may see a Vigil hideout when its owner says yes: the owner's choice). The owner's card shows ACCEPT / NO for 30 s, one card at a time per owner, one open ask per visitor; NO means a 60 s wait for that pair, KICK 5 min; at most 4 visitors in one HQ. Fixed lines only (Config.Hq.visit.text and reasons); names are hero names or display names, as the squad invite.
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 mutes and blocks): your own mute (the save's SettingsRules list) or block refuses your VISIT ("Unmute or unblock them first"). The owner's mute, Roblox block or report on you hides your ask: you read "sent", they never see it, and you are never told (as with PvP rematches). Muting or blocking during a visit sends the visitor home within a second ("Your visit is over"). Blocks: the device sends StarterGui GetBlockedUserIds at start and after each block or unblock (GuardBlocks, { 0 } = none, 200 at most); GuardService.shuns(a, b) = a blocked b or reported b on this server (GuardRules.reported). It only narrows who reaches a player; it never punishes.
- 2026-11-18 (WP12 remotes and analytics): HqVisitAsk 0.5/s, HqVisitAnswer, HqVisitKick 1/s, HqVisitLeave 0.5/s, GuardBlocks 0.2/s; user ids and 0/1 only, no strings. One event, hq_visit (the owner's side, same or cross side, visitors in that HQ; value: the owner's HQ level), at most once a minute per visitor, so at most 1 a minute a player against a budget of 20 a player.
- 2026-11-18 (side quest juice pass, overnight item 7): every moment of all 16 chains fires a juice row (7 new rows in Config.Audio.juice: questAccept, questStep, questVillain, questEncore, questFinale, questDone, questRetry; the map from moment and step kind in Config.Audio.sideQuest, read by Shared.QuestJuice). StoryHud fires them from the side quest toasts it already gets (no new remote): NEW QUEST! on accept, the step kind's word stamp with STEP n OF m and the pay on each step, the name card's sting, ENCORE!, VICTORY! with fireworks on the finale's win, then the chain's result card (its name COMPLETE!, the pay, one line per cosmetic: NEW TITLE, NEW STYLE by its Gear name) with confetti and the music's victory sting, and TRY AGAIN! when Downed while a finale villain stands (the finale's only loss: the villain waits). Your side's part done with the other side still working is a soft chime and ring, no stamp. Stamp words are fixed UI words, no story text. Sounds are built-in clips (PLACEHOLDER SOUND).
- 2026-11-18 (side quest juice pass): the sqStep and sqDone toasts carry the pay (credits, renown) for the stamp and the card, and a side quest pocket run's MissionResult carries its chain's bit (sq), so the result card names the chain instead of a blank mission with "Replay: first-clear rewards already earned". The mission result card's look is unchanged (its line drawing is shared: StoryHud fillCard). AudioController.victory plays the victory sting only once it has an id, so the patrol music no longer dips for a silent sting.
- 2026-11-18 (city audit follow-up, bug): fast-travel beacon 2 (Downtown East) stood on the roof of building 87, which could collapse and leave the pad floating until repair. A beacon's host building is now fixed: WorldMap marks it (Building.fixed) and BuildingState never breaks a fixed building (blast, punch, hit and force do nothing; a knockback never picks its wall). Same rule as the safehouses, whose roofs are no wreckable building at all; building ids do not move. Only building 87 is a beacon host today (the other beacons stand on the Beacon Tower, the crane, the clock, landmarks, safehouse roofs and the windmill, none wreckable).
- 2026-11-18 (city audit follow-up): a side quest goal on a roof (a hold or reach on a roof, a carry's roof pad, a rooftop race's or chase's course roofs) holds its buildings whole only while the goal stands (BuildingState.pin and unpin, counted per holder; BuildingService.pin), and a roof spot is only ever picked on a standing building. When the goal ends the roof breaks like any other, so "every damaging ability breaks buildings" still holds everywhere else. The 6-roof prologue chase row keeps nate's rule (real buildings that wreck and repair).
- 2026-11-18 (text pass): house spelling for player text is British (colour, harbour, honour, favourite, centre, theatre, tyre) except armor, armored (the established ability and creator piece names and nate's story lines); Brightharbor stays one proper name. So Heavy Armour, Armour Plates and two "armoured" lines read armor, Mentors' "flier" reads flyer (as every other line), and the Docks beacon "Harbor Gantry" reads "Harbour Gantry" (display name only, like Gantry Yard). A test (tests/text_test.luau) holds the spellings.
- 2026-11-18 (text pass): players never read the internal word "mission pocket": the busy-missions toast and the Task Force board say "Too many missions running: try again in a minute". Tab hints name tabs as shown (GEAR > STYLES, not "Show Style" or "Styles"); loading notes end in "..."; "at your HQ or a safehouse" everywhere; side-vs-side banners read THE VIGIL WINS! / THE SYNDICATE WINS! and the Uptown vault line "The Syndicate wants" (one side, singular).
- 2026-11-18 (text pass): the Core recovery tracker said "shard" (old lore, Decisions 2026-11-17 step 1); it says "core". The level 21 creator pants are shown as Genie Pants (were Harem Pants; key harem, id and unlock unchanged, so saved looks keep them). The Cooling Kill perk's text reads "Each defeat refunds 20% of your super's cooldown" (the game says defeat, not kill); its name is nate's call.
- 2026-11-18 (text pass): tests/text_test.luau (ONLY="Text pass") reads every Config string and every device-code literal (src/client, src/first; comments and "[Service]" log lines skipped) for old names (Marlo, Captain Gleam, Shardfall, Comet, Null, League, War-Frame, Spark Trooper/Drone, Crane Yard, Harbor Crane, Mayor's Sash, Seven League, shard crate/recovery, the old Act 1 mission names, the retired arcs' people, nate's other games), the fixed wordings, the house spellings, common misspellings, repeated words, stray or missing spaces at punctuation and lowercase game terms (credits, renown, vigil, syndicate, foundry, lumen dust, skill point, hero pass, rogues file, infamy); server and shared literals for old names. Story, Cinema and side quest lines are scanned but were not reworded (nate's drafts; none had a typo).
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding check, overnight item 8): the first 60 s are a pure sim (tests/onboarding_sim.luau on the real numbers; ONLY="Onboarding"): no stretch over 3 s with nothing to do or watch, a goal on screen in every beat, control within 30 s of the prologue starting and first_spark within 40 s for the nominal player. Before: control at 38 s, first_spark at 43 s; after: 29.5 s and 35 s.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, spec number): the ceremony beat 30 -> 22 s and the scan 8 -> 7.5 s, the same lines re-timed to their reading time (Shared.GuidePlan.duration); the rescue 9 -> 13 s because its three lines (Lodestar, Patch, Lodestar) could not be read in 9 (one showed 1.5 s of 3). No line was added, removed or reworded. The camera shots are shares of the beat, so they follow.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding): the prologue's verbs moved to Config.Ui.prologue.verbs (StoryClient.VERBS reads them) and each beat with a place to reach shows one fixed word under its gold marker (RUN, SPARK, LEAP, LEAP, SUBWAY, RUN; Config.Ui.prologue.goals). The run beat pulsed LEAP while asking you to run and its line ("Please remain calm!") gave no goal. These are the only new words; the camera beats stay wordless.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding): beat lines are timed on the beat's clock, so a quick player lost them (a Leap at once ended the leap beat in about 3 s: Patch's "I'm Patch. I hacked your phone." never played). A beat that ends before its lines are heard (shown under 70% of their reading time) hands at most 2 of them to the next player-paced beat, which plays them first and then its own, none cutting another short (GuidePlan.unheard and withCarry; Config.Ui.prologue.carry and heard). Camera and timed beats keep their own timing and take nothing.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, bug): a missed hop in the leap or chase left the hero in the street with no way back up until the beat's stuck timer (chase 90 s). Now a hero more than Config.Story.opening.fall.drop (10) studs below the lower of the two roofs for fall.after (1.2 s) is put back on the last roof reached, facing the next; a respawn mid-chase also lands there (was the chase's first roof).
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, bug): the mentor picker popped over the title card (the title pays level 3, and HeroService.Leveled asked for the picker); MentorService.maybeAsk now waits while the prologue runs, so Config.Mentors.afterOpening (8 s after the title) is what opens it.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding): the cards the server pops by itself (the daily claim card, the mentor picker, the TOP 10 board card) wait on the device until the player is free: no loading screen, main menu, creator, prologue or cutscene, and no other such card open (Ui.PopupGate; Config.Ui.popups: 1.2 s of calm, at most 600 s, then it shows anyway). After the prologue the daily card comes first, then the picker. The board card no longer pops under the loading screen or the main menu on join.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, bug): the daily card's 600 s wait for a free hero counted the creator and the prologue, so a slow creator plus the prologue could end a new player's first session with no card; that time no longer counts (the reward was never lost: it waits for CLAIM).
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, bug): the main menu showed PLAY, SHOP and SETTINGS over a rejoin mid-prologue (the client saw only `created`), and on a returning account it hid CREATE FIRST, so the held ceremony waited for good. The client view now carries `opening` (no save change): the returning menu needs the prologue done, and Create First may replace it. A held ceremony also gives up after Config.Story.opening.holdMax (120 s) and takes the Create First path, rewards included.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding): controllers: the main menu and the creator select their first button as they open, and gamepad Select goes back into an open one instead of the hidden top row (Kit.padFocus, Kit.modalButton); SKIP reads SKIP (B) on a controller and B presses it (skip never skips a reward: the prologue's rewards land at the title, and a skipped title still pays through finish). The cards that pop mid-play register for Select but do not take the stick.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding): the loading screen stays up until the save is on the hero (HeroService sets the HeroLoaded attribute; still never past 12 s) and says LOADING THE CITY, then LOADING YOUR HERO, so a new player no longer stands on a bare HUD until the creator opens.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, analytics): spark_detected, first_spark and fake_cracked now carry seconds since the prologue started as their value (was seconds since joining, which counted the creator), so the Soft Launch KPI "first_spark within 60 s of the prologue starting" reads straight off the event.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, bug): with every subway lane busy the fight was placed back at the plaza start (OpeningPlan's lane-less point); OpeningService now uses the subway steps for the tunnel, fight, swarm and rescue then, as the 2026-11-17 decision said.
- 2026-11-18 (onboarding, coordinator's item): the prologue's rooftop row is ordinary wreckable Downtown buildings, so other players could knock out a roof a new hero was about to land on. While at least one prologue is on the row's beats (Config.Story.opening.holdRow: leap, chase and the suit on the last roof) the row's six buildings are held whole through the side quest roofs' holder-counted pin (BuildingService.pin, Shared.PrologueRow); each run lets go as it moves to the subway, finishes, stops or leaves, so the row breaks again once no prologue is on it. A roof already wrecked before a prologue reaches it is not repaired by this (see the open question).

There is no "Halcyon Guardian" robot on this base (no type or dress by that name), so none was drawn.

- 2026-11-18 (calls batch, interim pick, nate to OK): the badge "Bottom Toucher" is shown as "Deep Diver" and the perk "Cooling Kill" as "Cooling Down" (display names only: badge id trenchfloor, its trenchFloor trigger, bit and title, and the perk key CoolingKill that gear saves and drops name, its 20% refund and half in PvP are unchanged, so no save or unlock moves). tests/text_test.luau bans both old names in every Config string and code literal. Undo: the name line in Config.Badges / Config.Gear.perks.CoolingKill and the two OLD_NAMES rows.
- 2026-11-18 (calls batch, interim pick, nate to OK; answers the analytics audit's "A Rogues page event is nate's call"): one new event, rogues_page (page kind: robots, fakes, villains or bosses; gold 0/1; value: pages filled on the account), sent when RoguesService first fills a page (RoguesFile.beat's fresh, Shared.RoguesFile.event). No names, ids or typed text. Once per page per account because the page's bit and log row are saved (a rejoin or a server hop loads them back: nothing again; a save that did not load sends nothing, like every non-NOSAVE event): at most 48 in an account's life (45 pages, 3 gold). It goes through Analytics.event, so it takes a slot in the sliding 60 s window like every send; it is not low priority, and the biggest burst (30 players beating a boss at the gold level: 60 at once) fits beside the busy events' half (cap 720). A full window keeps it queued for the session and it goes with the next 5 s try (RoguesFile.drain; Analytics.event now returns "sent", "full" or "refused", which other callers ignore). Undo: Config.Rogues.analytics = false. Tests: ONLY="Rogues File".
- 2026-11-18: tests/run.sh v2: a frozen Lune is spotted by memory flat for 25 s (not a fixed timeout), runs at nice -10, pauses Blender/bpy renders while it runs, 8 tries; TRACE=1 prints each test's name. Busy CPUs (renders) are what freeze Lune 0.8.9; a newer Lune is a nate call (rokit pin).
