- 2026-11-18 (nate's playtest: "the prologue needs to be more scripted so even a little kid could easily do it ... events and spawns happen during cutscenes and mid mission"): the prologue is kid-proof. The hero is alone with its story: its robots are solo (EnemySim's solo rule: they see and hurt only that hero, only it can hurt them, no other robot, shot or blast reaches it), and the device hides the live world for the whole prologue (other heroes, their plates and casts, city robots and their shots, city citizens; no device scene cuts in). Roof hops are guided (one Leap press on a roof is a set arc to the next roof, and from the last roof down to the subway steps; the server grants the travel). The tunnel caves in behind the hero instead of on a timer (no knock-backs). Skylance and the swarm can't leave the subway chamber (Enemy.bounds: no more dashing through walls). Every played beat shows one plain instruction with the device's key ("Press SPACE to jump to the next roof!"). Patch's swarm line says "Security bots" instead of "Crane robots" so the machine reveal stays hidden.
- 2026-11-18 (nate's playtest: "Enemies just go through walls... the subway fight is impossible"): the subway pocket never told the robots its walls (EnemyService answered a flat floor), so Skylance kept range by walking out of the chamber. EnemyService.usePocket now holds every built pocket's layout (the subway and mission runs) with its ceilings (PocketLayout.ceilingAt); every robot move, knock, pull, nudge, dash and shot is checked a stud apart to the body's edge (EnemySim.groundClear, flyClear), so thin walls hold at any speed; fliers slide along walls and climb over buildings instead of popping onto roofs; a kiter backed into a wall fights (cornered); the subway swarm spawns inside the chamber (swarm.margin 5). tests/walls_test.luau.
- 2026-11-18 (nate: "Jumping with superspeed needs to be more fluid, like holding the jump without losing speed"): Super-speed's running jump (Move.mobility.speed.hop: from 30, up 45, turnRate 160): Leap while running is an instant hop at the run's speed (no crouch), the air keeps the speed while steering, holding Leap hops again on landing, landing keeps the sprint; only a real fall (below -60) glides. The speed check cap includes the hop's lift. tests/hop_test.luau.
- 2026-11-18 (nate: "animations for all attacks/powers... my character should have an animation"): each power path has its own whole-body moves by shape group (HeroAnim.PATH_CLIPS; 11 new clips with stance, anticipation and follow-through); Blade keeps its sword swings, weapons their sets; an arm-only cast while standing gets a leg brace. Still hand-keyed PLACEHOLDER ANIMATION; real uploaded animations (Moon Animator 2 or Blender to a KeyframeSequence) are a later art pass. tests/anim_test.luau.
- 2026-11-18 (nate called the UI "AI slop" and picked the bold comic mockup B): the comic UI look, in Kit so every screen gets it: Kit.ink gives drawn panels and buttons a 3 px ink outline and a hard 4 px ink drop shadow (two ink bars outside the bottom and right edges in a Folder "Ink", since a child always draws over its parent and a Folder is outside UIListLayout), round buttons and the radar a round sibling shadow (Kit.discInk; Super on a red starburst), cream paper (255,247,224) and navy (31,42,68) panels with faint halftone dots (art/ui/halftone.py, uploaded image 89303571654854), corners 4 px, light Bangers text outlined in ink, tabs and toasts tilted 1.5 degrees, toasts as speech bubbles, a striped health bar. Config.Ui.ink holds the numbers; ink = false opts out; square frames (round = false) are shapes and stay plain. Layout, sizes and text unchanged. tests/uishot.luau + uishot.py render the real UI to PNG without Studio.
- 2026-11-19 (nate: "vehicles look like trash": plain or grey, too toy-like): the grey came from the art itself: the old vehicle meshes had no UVs, texture or material, so each was one flat SmoothPlastic colour from Config.VehicleArt (and the Ashgrove tug's towed car was two plain boxes). All 12 looks are remade as sleek cartoon vehicles with a baked texture set each (art/blender/vkit.py; SurfaceAppearance, AlphaMode Overlay: the paint texels are see-through so VehicleKit's tint still paints the body); SetPieceMeshes keeps a fit's `maps = true` SurfaceAppearance on the clone (it used to strip every child); the towed car wears the sedan look. Budgets: about 6k triangles a car, under about 10k a tram, truck or escort (all 40 hub vehicles about 265k; Roblox's automatic mesh LOD thins them at range). tests/vehicles_test.luau "every look is textured".
- 2026-11-19 (nate: "wayyy too much on screen, especially the bar at the top with buttons"): the declutter pass. The top row keeps POWERS, GEAR, MAP, SHOP (and LEAVE on a visit, PVP while flagged via the DockPin attribute); every other screen button (JOURNAL, SQUAD, EMOTE, PING, SETTINGS, PASS, DAILY, BOARDS, PHOTO, MENTOR ...) is in a 2-column MENU grid in its own screen (DisplayOrder 4, over the action buttons, under panels); "MENU !" in gold when a drawer item has something to claim; any panel opening shuts the grid; hotkeys and gamepad Select are unchanged (Config.Ui.dock). Status card: the Awakening meter row shows only once it holds a charge. Action buttons: locked skill slots hide; with mouse and keyboard the movement buttons (LEAP, FLY, BOOST, DOWN) become one key line under the skills and the shield closes up beside them (touch and gamepad keep the buttons; the opening's focus verbs still show).
- 2026-11-19 (nate: "so many buttons are extremely unreliable, next button in character creator still requires me to keep hitting it"): causes found in code: (1) the HUD's top-row buttons press on InputBegan and let go only on their own InputEnded, so with a mouse the release landed on the screen the press opened, the action stayed down, and the next click was no press (every other click did nothing); now a mouse press lets go wherever the button comes up (InputController.holdMouse). (2) Kit.pending kept TRY AGAIN locked for 1.6 s, so the tap it asked for did nothing; it now takes a tap at once. (3) Kit.tapGuard: every Kit button also counts a press that starts and ends on it (within 14 px, not a scroll) when no Activated came within 0.12 s, deduped per press, so a dropped Activated never loses a tap (Config.Ui.tap). Tested in uisim: hit tests that a tap on every shown button's middle reaches it (HUD, MENU grid, creator) and that ink never takes input.
- 2026-11-19 (nate's playtest 2: "Npcs and enemies still don't wear clothes properly", "there are still npcs that don't look like Roblox avatars"): every NpcRig (robots, citizens, givers, helpers, mentors, the prologue crowd) is built on Roblox's own blocky R15 meshes (HeroBody publishes ReplicatedStorage.RobloxBody) with an inert Humanoid (EvaluateStateMachine off, no name or health) so real Shirt and Pants composite on the meshes' own UVs like the heroes'. Roblox projects Textures on MeshParts flat onto the box faces, which is why the painted-on clothes never fit. Each side by side clothes image got its Shirt and Pants halves uploaded (208 images, Config.ClothesSplit). Faces are Roblox catalog faces (a robot that turns shows glowing eye parts, not a drawn turned face) and hair is catalog hair (NpcRig.showHair; citizens in 6 hair groups so a citizen looks the same on every device). In NPC looks, the old drawn hair and faces become a catalog hair and face picked by the old piece's name, and the retired eyes, beard, face and makeup tabs are not drawn. The hit flash on clothed robots is a pooled white Highlight (6 at most). Config.NpcLooks.robloxBody = false goes back.
- 2026-11-19 (creator NEXT "requires me to keep hitting it"): no overlap found (headless hit test at 1080p, 1366 and phone sizes); the creator now draws each step and the preview under pcall, so one error cannot leave the old step showing, and a profile sync waits for a held press before rebuilding the lists. The shared Kit.button press fix is the UI thread's (quality-ui2).

ImpactService: rams at 85+ studs/s smash walls (hit-stop, shake, 30% speed loss, server-checked against the hero's own speed), robot hits over 12% max health and boss strikes throw heroes through walls, robots knocked 8+ studs into walls break them, the pound breaks roofs; pins, scenes, travel and safe areas never break; caps 6 per hero per 4 s, 12 a second a server (Config.Combat.impact).

- 2026-11-19 (gap-icons): the icon set is drawn in code in the pure comic UI style (art/icons/icons.py with ink.py and symbols.py, plain python3 + PIL/numpy, deterministic): a power = its path's disc (path colour, the element motif tone on tone, halftone, ink ring; supers a gold ring with studs) + a symbol for what it does, no two powers in a path sharing one (the script stops otherwise); a weapon move = the weapon's silhouette on its family colour + a move badge (combo, 1 to 4 pips) laid on by the UI, so 36 + 5 cells cover 180 moves; gear = slot symbol over a tier frame (tier colour plus 1 to 6 pips, so colourblind players read it; studs from Rare, rays on Legendary and Mythic). - 2026-11-19 (comic art pass, nate: "a more cinematic game"): the 64 comic page panels (Config.Cinema.comic.pages) and the 50 guide, giver and mentor portraits are rendered art (Config.ComicArt, generated by art/panels/post.py; Shared.ComicArt picks the image): each staged in Blender (art/blender/panels.py + scenes.py, one staging per panel) on Roblox's blocky R15 body in the person's game outfit (uploaded clothes, else the creator look's colours, else a picked hero shirt and pants) with the look's pieces, then inked (Freestyle + a PIL outline, halftone, ink frame). Panels are one 1024 x 512 image each (cropped to each panel's shape by the UI); portraits are 256 px cells, 16 to a 1024 sheet (4 sheets). Captions, sound words, timings and SKIP stay in the UI; an id of 0 keeps today's frame drawing.
- 2026-11-19 (comic art pass, nate's catalog rule): every rendered face and hair is the Roblox catalog item itself: face images and hair accessories (mesh + texture, fitted by HairAttachment) fetched through Open Cloud asset delivery by art/panels/catalog.py into build/catalog (not in git). A person with a creator look wears the same catalog face and hair the game gives that look (LookKit's catalogFaceFor/catalogHairFor by the old piece's name), so the art matches the world; the hashed picks are sometimes a poor fit (e.g. Lodestar's blonde ponytail, the Pitboss's long hair): nate may want "cat:" face and hair picks on those looks (would change both game and art). People with no look got picks: Patch Check It + Pal Hair, Crane Know-It-All Grin + Brown Charmer Hair, the insider Stare (hood), the copy Sinister + Black Short Wolf Cut, the Toymaker Glee + Lavender Updo, Lark Bandage + Blonde Action Ponytail, Wren Smiling Girl + Black Ponytail, Toby Happy D + Cinnamon Hair, Flint Beast Mode + Crimson Shaggy 2.0, Finn Joyful Smile + Blonde Spiked Hair, Dace Chill + Short Blue Hair, Ambrose Tired Face + Long Pastel Hair, Hoshi Alright + Black Royal Braid, Captain Meridian Man Face + Clean Shiny Spikes, Old Beacon Friendly Grin + Hair Base In Brown, The Margrave Take Over the World + Purple and Blue Male Hair Slick, The Velvet Paw Slickfang + Black Trendy Messy Buns (art/panels/cast.luau FACE/HAIR). Portrait extras that were facial hair or scars are dropped (no catalog beards yet); hats cut the hair under their band. The Dev panel's SampleGiver keeps the drawn portrait.

- 2026-11-19 (pt-bosses, nate's playtest "Boss is stuck between building"): a walking boss smashes through the buildings it walks into (BossSim smash events -> BuildingService.smash: 2,400 structure damage at its feet every 0.5 s a building, inside the 3-collapse cap) and is slowed to 60% while plowing; pinned buildings (beacon hosts, side quest roofs) push it round instead; after 4 s making no progress toward its crowd it leaps (telegraphed) to open ground near it. Rustjaw's footprint is 14 studs so it walks the 30-stud streets; it now keeps walking while its cannons fire (only its own wind-ups hold it) and charges at 2x past 120 studs.
- 2026-11-19 (pt-bosses, nate: "a lot of bosses I can just fly stepping with ranged moves"): every boss and the War-Frame got 3 reactive counters on top of the old rotation (never two counters in a row): homing shots at airborne heroes away from it (follow 60% of a move, at most 40 studs: moving dodges them), a column swat at heroes hovering by its body (knocks them down), and a gap closer on a hero more than kiteRange away (or above its top) for 10 s (Rustjaw: Leap; the others: a tether that yanks the hero in to 30 studs); nobody close for 25 s enrages it (gaps x0.7, hits x1.25). Melee heroes on the ground are never a counter's target. Sim (party of 4, level 20, 50% dodges): fliers went from 0 to 0.29-0.53 of their HP a minute, melee unchanged (0.68-0.94), both win every boss in 72-162 s.

- 2026-11-19 (fix-anims, nate after gaps7: "there just still aren't animations for attacks or powers or anything"): root cause found in the runtime, not the clips. Roblox's Avatar Joint Upgrade (StarterPlayer.AvatarJointUpgrade, "the default for new experiences") spawns R15 player characters with AnimationConstraints instead of Motor6Ds; our place never set the property (Rojo wrote nothing, so it was on Default), and HeroAnimator, EmoteFx and NpcGestures only collected Motor6Ds, so on nate's hero HeroAnimator.rigOf found no Waist and every cast, windup, swing, mode pose and emote silently did nothing, while Roblox's walk (the Animator) and robots (NpcRig's own Motor6Ds) still moved. Fix, both halves: (1) default.project.json pins StarterPlayer.AvatarJointUpgrade_Serialized = Disabled (the property is RobloxScriptSecurity, so only the place file can set it; checked in the built rbxl), so heroes keep Motor6Ds and every Motor6D path (EmoteFx's C0 tweens, LookKit's refit) works as built; (2) Shared.RigJoints finds joints of both kinds by R15 name (by child part when named otherwise), HeroAnimator, EmoteFx and NpcGestures use it, and an emote on AnimationConstraints (read-only C0) goes through HeroAnimator.emotePose on Transform instead, so a forced upgrade later cannot break animation again. Kept the code-driven Transform clips rather than uploaded Animation assets: Transform written in PreSimulation is after the Animator by Roblox's documented order (Animator after PreAnimation, Transform applied after PreSimulation) and works on both joint kinds, needs no uploads and keeps the 300+ clips data-driven; uploaded KeyframeSequences would be a second copy of every clip for no gain here. HeroAnimator prints "[HeroAnimator] your hero: N joints (kind)" once per spawn and warns if your hero has no Waist joint after 5 s. Test: tests/fix_anims_test.luau boots the real client from the built place (UiSim), presses Basic through InputController and CombatClient.fire, fires PreSimulation and reads the joints back, on Motor6D, AnimationConstraint and odd-named rigs; on the gaps7 place it fails exactly as nate saw (0 degrees on upgraded joints).

Now every popup goes through Ui.Dismiss: an X (a round 30 px X face, 22 px on the trackers, on a clear 40 px tap area inside the corner, Config.Ui.phone.minTap; none added where the popup already has X, CLOSE, NO, NO THANKS, LATER, NICE! or OK; none on a toast, too short for a phone's 40 px target: the whole bubble is its tap) and a tap anywhere on it (a clear button under its content, so its own buttons answer first; not on the cards that scroll or hold a list or pages: daily, mentor picker, episode, contest vote), Backspace clears every popup on screen and the waiting line (the answer to your own tap stays). B was asked for but is taken: keyboard B is GRAB and the prompts' key, the pad's B is Down; on a pad the short lives do it.

The contest banner folds 12 s after each new step (Config.Contest.bannerLife) and has its X in every step. The corner TV has an X and a tap (Crane's next segment then waits twice as long). The world tracker and the mission and side quest trackers have an X (no tap: mostly clear screen) that folds them until something new joins (a new row, the next step); the story choice card never folds (it waits for its answer).

- 2026-11-19 (fix-carry, nate after gaps7: "when i pickup cars or items or whatever i should actually hold them. Unless i have powers like telekinesis then i could make them float"): root cause: ThrowService welded every carried thing to the HumanoidRootPart 4.5 studs up, about 2.1 studs above the raised fists (pt-anims' carry pose even had the hands behind and below the head top), and a held live robot was only a server position sent at 10 Hz and drawn 0.1 s behind (about 8 studs back at flight speed) while tumbling. Now Shared.CarryPose works out the hold from the arm's forward kinematics on the character's own joints (Motor6D or AnimationConstraint via Shared.RigJoints): big things (cars, trucks, slabs, dumpsters, vending machines, wrecks, live robots) on both raised fists, welded to the UpperTorso; small ones (bench, bin, mailbox, rubble) up on the right fist and poles (sign, lamp post) gripped upright, welded to the RightHand (Config.Throw.kinds grip, Config.Throw.hold).
- 2026-11-19 (fix-carry): server-owned Welds into the character, not a client-only visual weld: the weld joins the hero's assembly (massless, no collisions), the hero's device owns the assembly's physics and every device solves the joints from the arm poses it draws itself (HeroAnimator), so it sits in the hands on every screen with no lag and no extra traffic, and the server keeps authority (grabs, throws, hits). Live robots are no parts, so each device draws a held robot from the same CarryPose sums at its carrier's hands (EnemyRenderer.heldAt, placed again after physics so it never trails), and the server keeps the robot's middle there for the hits.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-carry): telekinesis paths are Gravity (Pulse: "floats citizens and objects") and Mind (psychic, its own Telekinesis power); Spirit (a guardian summoner) and Light hold things like everyone (Config.Throw.mind.paths). Their things float 1.6 studs over the head, bob 0.3 studs, glow in the path's colour (a Highlight and a light, server instances) with both hands raised (carryMind); a swing whirls them round the hero on every device.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-carry): pt-anims' carry clip is re-keyed (arms straight up, fists just over the head: the blocky arm reaches only 0.2 studs over the head top) and two hold clips added (carryHand, carryMind). A live robot is held overhead like everything big (was CarryGrip "club", robotHold); during the lift and the robot swing, backhand and slam clips the held thing follows the right hand on every device (ThrowClient, CarryPose.FOLLOW), so swings come from the clips instead of the server's replicated weld tween.

- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy, nate after gaps7: "I want to fly straight through buildings"; "MORE DESTRUCTION. Why cant i destroy silos? I literally want everything destructible even terrain"): every built body is a wreck record now: the outskirts' cylinders (WorldMap.roundBody: about 340 silos with their caps, about 100 lighthouses with their bands and lamps, the beacon windmill with its roof and blades) and the hub's last whole bodies (WorldMap.moreHub: the Foundry and its 3 stacks, the clock tower, the 16 safehouse shelters), ids after every other record (2,485 -> about 3,000 a week, under the snapshot's 4,096). Round bodies keep the Structure grid and supports but each piece is drawn on the circle (Structure.roundOff: 4 diagonal pillars, 2 turned chord panels a face, the slab a disc); the device learns which are round from WorldInfo's 8th field and the snapshot's rd.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy): pins are shells, not whole: a fixed or pinned building (beacon hosts and everything within 14 studs of a pad or beacon, side quest roofs and the prologue row while held, the Foundry as the Syndicate's way in, the windmill, the clock tower, safehouse shelters) takes holes in its walls and inner floors but never loses a pillar or its roof and no storey gives way (Structure.blast/settle shell), then the drones mend it as usual; rams and drops break them as shells too (BuildingService.breakable). Only the shelter's body takes a record, so its roof ledge, pad, terminals and respawns never move. Kept whole on purpose: the Docks crane (a beacon stands on it; 4-stud legs make no pieces), the Crater's lab ruins (already ruins, Blender looks over hidden boxes, the PvP Crater's cover), landmark rock, dunes, piers, boardwalks, a fountain basin and lighthouse rock feet (ground: they crater instead), mission and side quest pocket props (mission geometry; breaking it could block a step).
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy): the street breaks more: a device knocks loose anything up to 32 studs tall and 20 across (Shared.StreetBreak: containers, hay, windmill poles, tractors as well as trees, lamps and benches) and a long fence breaks only where it is hit (6-stud stand-in lengths, the ones in reach fly), back after 45 s as before; a hero flying through one knocks it loose without stopping (MoveController.onPropHit) and ImpactProp passes the knock to the players near (BuildingImpact kind 3).
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy, spec change): structural damage 1.5x easier everywhere at once: Config.Destruction.structure.chunkCost 600 -> 400 (a super's 3,000 knocks out about 7 pieces, a skill's 700 1 to 2, 2 basics a piece instead of 3) and maxPerHit 10 -> 15. Structure HP still follows the weight lost; collapse cap (3 in 6 s), falling and debris caps per preset unchanged.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy, spec change): flying through: Config.Combat.impact.ram minSpeed 85 -> 45 (plain flight at 60 rams; glide and swoops ram too), fullSpeed 160 -> 140, dmg {900, 3000} -> {1600, 4000} (a hero-sized hole of 4 to 10 pieces), radius 9 -> 10, loss 0.3 -> 0.1, hitStop 0.08 -> 0.05 s keeping 25% (was 8%), device cooldown 0.3 -> 0.12, sameWall 0.6 -> 0.25 (the far wall), perHero 6 -> 12 a 4 s, serverPerSecond 12 -> 20, ImpactRam rate 4 -> 8/s. The hole opens on the device at once (BuildingFx.predict: the same Structure.blast the server runs; its pieces fly ahead of the hero without colliding for 0.35 s) and a predicted piece the server never confirms comes back after 1.5 s. A ram only ever lowers the speed, so MoveGuard never pulls a hero back for it.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy): ground craters (Shared.CraterRules, CraterService, Fx.GroundFx): a hit of 1,500+ structure damage at the ground (supers, boss slams, pounds, big drops onto the ground) digs one 5 to 12 studs across. The city's ground is parts and Terrain exists only under the water (the seabed and the ring's rock), so Terrain is dug for real (ReadVoxels, carve, WriteVoxels; a Water voxel is never touched and a voxel dug under the water's surface becomes Water, so no water volume opens) and written back as read when it heals; part ground (roads, sidewalks, plazas, fields, sand, piers) gets the drawn crater on every device from the server's list (the ink decal, a dark pit, rim chunks in the ground's colour that sink back while healing). Life 240 s, the last 60 healing; caps 3 a second, 2 a 48-stud cell, 48 live, terrain craters never overlap. A device draws its preset's craters nearest (6/12/20) with craterRim chunks each (6/10/14). The old 30 s client crater on BuildingImpact kind 1 gives way to these.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-destroy, nate: "billboard words aren't lined up properly on the billboards, and they keep showing after the building is destroyed"): the news screens' words lose the comic ink's hard-shadow bars (they hung off each label's box on the 3D screen), the ticker is clipped to the screen's face (ClipsDescendants), and the channel mark is placed back out over the band's left padding (UIPadding moved it onto the words). A screen falls (NewsHud drops a dark copy) once any wall piece it hangs on is knocked out, not only on a collapse (NewsPlan.behind, screenDown; checked every second). Every Decal, Texture, SurfaceGui and BillboardGui on a building's parts hides with the body on the device (BuildingFx.setHidden), and a flung sign keeps its words as it falls.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-weapons, nate after gaps7: "weapons STILL AREN'T PROPERLY MADE. why are there just pieces floating around?"): root cause is the upload, not the art or the welds. Open Cloud's FBX import turned every weapon mesh 180 degrees about y inside its MeshPart (x and z flipped; box size and centre unchanged), measured on all 259 downloaded MeshIds (art/upload/meshcheck.py). Each piece sat in the right box facing backwards, so blades, hafts and handles drawn on a diagonal crossed the wrong way: 194 of 720 pieces sat over 0.5 stud (up to 2.9) from the Blender layout and 17 of the 36 weapons came apart. Fixed in placement, no re-upload: Shared.MeshFix.turn("Weapons") (CFrame.Angles(0, pi, 0)) is applied to each mesh WeaponKit welds; sets not verified stay identity (fix-models adds the others). Welds, flags and swaps were already right. Gloves, Capes and Heads measure the same turn (handed to fix-models). Re-run meshcheck after any re-upload: a different importer may turn differently.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-weapons): the weapons are checked as the game builds them: tests/weaponsim.luau runs the place's own LookKit and WeaponKit on Roblox's blocky body headless (tests/weapon_sim_test.luau, ONLY="Weapon sim": 144 looks welded, turned, at their fit, no leftovers after swaps, the part fallback never beside meshes), and art/weapons/simdump.luau + simcheck.py + simrender.py lay Roblox's own downloaded geometry where the code puts it (0 pieces off, 0 looks apart; art/preview/weapons_ingame_before.png and _after.png). Mesh quality checked on that layout: every piece touches another of its weapon (0.05 stud on the faces), sizes match Config.WeaponArt exactly, so no mesh was remade (nate: the models are fine, the configuration was wrong).
- 2026-11-19 (fix-models, nate after gaps7: "a lot of our models don't look that bad they just aren't configured properly. Like the leviathan engine's parts just aren't placed properly ... consistent throughout the game"): same root cause as fix-weapons, for every set. art/upload/meshcheck.py run on all 39 other imported sets (2,303 meshes): every mesh matches Roblox's copy turned 180 degrees about y in its box, none matches as drawn (Hero's 15 are symmetric either way); per-set tally art/models/meshturns.json (art/upload/meshturns.py). Sizes and box centres were checked too (every Config.*Art size equals the imported MeshPart's size; MeshPart origin is the box centre as the Blender scripts assume), so no offset, pivot, axis or scale was wrong and no mesh was remade or re-uploaded. Fixed in placement: Shared.MeshFix (fix-weapons' file, one table) lists every measured set; MeshFix.clone tags a clone with its set (MeshSet attribute) and MeshFix.of(part) gives its turn, applied wherever a mesh is placed: WorldFx bosses, LookKit kits and capes, LookService gear, HqMeshes, SetPieceMeshes, CityProps and CityBuilder street art, StoryProps, PrologueMeshes, DeepMeshes (and the torpedo's flight), CraterDecor, PocketDecor, ActivityProps, ThrowService wrecks, NewsService screens, BoardService, EnemyRenderer's robot pieces. Welds hold the turn in C1. NpcRig turns a shell set's limbs (Trooper, finales, the HQ dummy) and gives their attachments and joints back in the drawn frame (C0/C1 = turn^-1 * drawn), so joint frames and every pose are unchanged; NpcRig.fix takes c0/c1 in the drawn frame, NpcRig.bend keeps the rest turn, NpcRig.face uses the Back face on a turned head. VehicleRig rolls a turned wheel about its drawn axle (it rolled backwards otherwise). tests/model_fit_test.luau builds every assembly with the game's code and fails on any mesh over 0.3 stud or 5 degrees from the Blender layout.
- 2026-11-19 (fix-bodies, after fix-flow put the Body step on Roblox's own body): LookKit.wearBodySet ApplyMesh-es the body sets' UpperTorso and LowerTorso onto the live torsos, and ApplyMesh cannot turn, so the upload's 180 degree turn (fix-models) would have put the female and Large/Titan torso detail on the back. Fixed at the source: art/blender/bodies.py --torsos writes art/export/BodyTorsos.fbx (the 12 torso meshes pre-turned 180 about y), uploaded from the cloud as Model 119292912239570 (Approved), pulled to art/models/BodyTorsos.rbxm (ids art/models/bodytorsos_meshids.json), ReplicatedStorage.Art.BodyTorsos. meshcheck against the drawn Bodies.fbx: identity, 12 of 12 as drawn (art/models/meshturns.json). LookKit's setMesh reads Art.BodyTorsos (no fallback to Art.Bodies: missing means Roblox's own torso stays); NpcRig keeps Art.Bodies with MeshFix's turn; BodyTorsos is not in MeshFix.TURNS (identity). Bodies.fbx itself is unchanged.

Acts 2 and 3 (12 more choices) will need saveRoom.choices raised past 12 then, with a ProfileSchema migration.

In each Syndicate pair, and the Vigil's Ceremony and Live pairs, one option pays the side's own track and the other City Reputation, so Act 1's least own-side total (Syndicate 570) and the rank pace stay put; both Vigil Safehouse options pay Trust (Vigil least 370 -> 420). The doc names no rewards or amounts: Claude's picks, for nate to OK.

A party member who did not pick (not the side's lead) hears the shared lines only.

He makes the Syndicate's Safehouse offer (Lodestar still lets them share the hideout). His portrait is rendered like the rest (art/panels; cast.luau puts late speakers last so every older portrait keeps its sheet cell).

- 2026-11-19 (move-2, nate on the card 20:45Z, spec change): the smash rule. Super-speed holding Boost at 45+ studs/s (mobility.speed.smash, the ram minimum) and a Leaping hero moving into a wall at 68+ studs/s flat (mobility.leap.smash.into: a charged running leap runs ~82, a tapped one ~60) go straight through a wreckable building like the flight ram (same ImpactRam path, Config.Combat.impact.ram, MoveGuard never pulls back: a ram only lowers speed). Super-speed's ram works on the ground too (MoveController.groundRam: a short hit-stop on the walk). Otherwise Super-speed runs up or along the wall (now with no Boost needed: the stick into the wall keeps the run going while stamina lasts) and Leaping grabs it. Anything that never breaks (no BuildingId) is climbed or run instead.
- 2026-11-19 (move-2, nate 20:42Z: "leap should have a wall climb similar to the old incredible hulk game"): Leaping's Hulk climb (mobility.leap.climb, Shared.ClimbMath): a leap, glide or fall into a wall, or Leap on the ground facing one within 4 studs, grabs it (mode climb); Leap bounds 30 studs straight up the face (mode bound, airGravity 120, sideways drift 14) to the next grab, holding Leap bounds again after a 0.14 s grip; past the top a vault (36 up, 24 in) onto the roof; Leap with the stick pulled away kicks off (58 out, 52 up); Down lets go. Each grab sends MoveGrab: ImpactService chips the building 120 (small hits add up until a piece breaks, BuildingState chip), once per 0.25 s, near the hero, never in safe areas. ~35 studs/s average climb; ranks: Hair Trigger grip 0.08, Skybound bounds 38. Every speed is inside the Leaping cap (the super leap's ~170), so no MoveCheck change.
- 2026-11-19 (move-2, nate 20:42Z: "whats the point of the player picking acrobat?"): Acrobat is the agility pick: wall crawl on any wall or ceiling (mode crawl: the face's own up and right from the camera, round outside and inside corners, onto a roof at the top, Boost scurries at 66 on 10 stamina/s, Leap jumps off with an air jump back, Down lets go), crawl 40 studs/s (the fastest climb: Leap's bounds average ~35, at rank 5 ~43 vs crawl 48), the best air control (air steer 95 / 46, Leaping 55 / 40), and dodge flips on Down (a 0.32 s tucked flip at 58 along the stick, back when still; MoveFlip opens a 0.3 s window on the server's own clock in which HeroService.damage misses every enemy, boss and PvP hit; cooldown 1.1 s, the server allows 15% for lag; DODGE! pops on a miss via MoveDodged). Head-on wall grabs crawl (was the climb straight up); glancing ones still wall run; double/triple jump and glide unchanged. Ranks: Spider Grip (crawl 48, wall runs 5 s), Spring Step (kept), Evasion (i-frames 0.4, cooldown 0.8), Freerunner (wall runs 54/6 s, glide 82, scurry 74, cap 88; Parkour and Updraft folded in), Triple Jump (+ cooldown 0.6).
- 2026-11-19 (move-2, nate 20:42Z: "make the swinging swing onto invisible things because if you aren't in the city its pretty useless"): swing anywhere. Outside the districts (SwingMath.inCity over Config.City.districts) a wider, lower fan (swing.wide: lifts 0.15/0.3/0.6, yaws to 90, near 10, 3 above) runs when the main one finds nothing, so trees, poles, rocks, cliffs and big props hold lines; with nothing at all the sky line (SwingMath.skyAnchor: 40 ahead and 34 up, only when the way up is open, never through a roof) swings on weaker numbers (top speed 78 vs 105, pump 18 vs 30, release cap 92 vs 115; SwingMath.skyConfig), and Leap on a sky line lets go (nothing to zip to). In the city the old search is unchanged (the street sim's chain kept 0.6+ of its speed only without the wide pass), so the city stays best. This reverses the 2026-11-19 "never a sky anchor" line for open ground only.
- 2026-11-19 (move-2, nate 20:46Z: "everyone should get a sprint with shift, superspeed is just way faster"): every pick sprints on Boost (Shift, LT, the touch SPRINT button, or the touch stick pushed to 95% of its edge) on the ground: the run eases from 20 to Config.Move.sprint.speed 28 (1.4x, eased at 8/s), Acrobat 24 to 32; no stamina (it is a jog, and stamina is for the mobility moves). caps.run is already 28 (and Acrobat's cap 75), so MoveCheck needed no change. Shift was already Boost and Roblox's Shift Lock is off (EnableMouseLockOption false), so no conflicts; L3 stays Swap on a gamepad (LT is Boost). The sprint stride is ClimbAnim.runFast past 25 studs/s; Super-speed keeps its own 100+ sprint and pose.
- 2026-11-20 (move-2, nate 00:03Z: "stamina/boost should refill quicker"): Config.Move.stamina regenGround 15 -> 40 (full 200 in 5 s, was 13 s) and regenAir 8 -> 20 (10 s), after regenDelay 0.6 s without a drain (boost, wall runs, the scurry; MoveMath.staminaStep's idle argument). MoveGuard's server mirror uses the same numbers and the same delay after a server-side boost drain; the HUD reads MoveController.stamina. Drains unchanged.
- 2026-11-19 (move-2): new modes climb, bound, crawl, flip and climbVault are appended to HeroAnim.MODES (20 in all; MoveMode now n(1, 20)); the vault is "climbVault" since HeroMoves already has a "vault" weapon move. The clips live in Shared.ClimbAnim (merged into HeroAnim.CLIPS with a no-clash assert), PLACEHOLDER ANIMATION like the rest; the flip turns the Root once (it shows on every device from the Move attribute). Existing juice sounds are reused (land for the grab, leap for each bound and the vault, boost for the flip) so the sounds branch can give them their own rows later.
- 2026-11-19 (sounds pass, nate: "need better sounds badly"): every sound is ours to ship: CC0 packs (Kenney, OpenGameArt/Freesound CC0 via EternityForest/Free-SFX, Meta's CC0 piano) layered with our own synth (low thump under impacts, rumble, air, bells, horns, sirens) by art/audio/build.py; music composed in art/audio/music.py. No CC BY (Meta's Oculus pack skipped), no Sonniss (host blocked and its licence bars raw redistribution). Kenney.nl, OpenGameArt and freesound.org themselves are blocked from the cloud, so the packs came from their CC0 GitHub mirrors (licence files checked).
- 2026-11-19 (sounds pass): sounds ship as 5 atlas uploads (Hits, World, Ui, Loops, Music) played by region (Sound.PlaybackRegion, LoopRegion for loops) instead of ~250 single uploads, because Roblox limits audio uploads a month; Config.AudioClips (generated) holds the regions, a row's `clip` (default its key) names its clip or variant stem, Shared.SoundPick resolves it; the built-in fallbacks stay for a row whose atlas has no id.
- 2026-11-19 (sounds pass): mix: sub-groups Combat, World, Ui, Ambience (and Voice) inside Effects under a gentle compressor (threshold -14, 3:1); music tracks in Music.Score; big booms (Config.Audio.mix.duck ids) duck the bed and music to 45% for 0.9 s; the same row starts at most once per 35 ms; big 3D sounds (range >= 200) stay full volume to 28 studs; 2D rows are never detuned (chimes stay in tune), 3D rows spread pitch 5% and volume 8%.
- 2026-11-19 (sounds pass): new movement sounds in Fx.MoveAudio (no change to movement): ground pound, awning bounce, splash, Acrobat dash, sonic boom at 95 studs/s (re-arms below 70, 4 s apart, with a 0.25 s shake) and the Super Speed rush loop; flight wind pitch now follows speed (speedPitch). The city bed (Config.Life.sounds.bed: Downtown, Uptown, OldTown, Docks, Outskirts, Rooftop) plays by the camera's district (Shared.LifePlan.districtAt), rooftop wind by height (60 to 260 studs), none underwater or above y 1,200.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn, nate: "my size reset" after a respawn): the server builds the look at once on Spawned, then again once the character is in Workspace (Roblox's spawn scaling resets part sizes on a body built before it is parented); LookService.guardShape watches the shaped parts' Size against their ShapedSize attribute and rebuilds (at most 6 times a life); the StarterCharacter's Humanoid.AutomaticScalingEnabled is off while the player's own avatar is off. Gear and the weapon are refitted after the shape every time.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn): NpcRig marks its models LaidOut so LookKit.build never shapes an NPC body twice.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn, nate: "Clothes dont fit on certain body sizes"): the body set torsos carry Roblox's own R15 torso UVs (art/blender/robloxuv.py copies them from Roblox's mesh, face by face, box to box), because Roblox composites classic Shirt and Pants into its own per-limb layout, not the classic template; our old torsos used template UVs, which scrambled clothes on every Female, Small, Large and Titan body. Re-uploaded as Model 138196936019402 (Approved; art/models/BodyTorsos.rbxm, meshcheck against Bodies.fbx identity 12/12; the uploaded UVs read Roblox's range u 0.006-0.994). Render check art/preview/fit_bodies_clothes.png (before: fit_bodies_before_clothes.png), from art/fit/bodies.luau + art/blender/fit_bodies.py.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn, nate: creator NEXT "super unresponsive", "keep spamming until it works"): cause: every NEXT press and every hover rebuilt the whole hero preview at once and any wallet sync redrew the whole creator, so frames hitched while pressing. Now Ui.Preview builds the latest ask once a frame and skips an unchanged look; hovers build after the pointer rests 0.08 s; NEXT, BACK and the step tabs fire on the press (onDown), light the next step at once and redraw a frame later; wallet-only syncs skip the redraw.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn): Kit watches for presses that something else drew over a button takes (GetGuiObjectsAtPosition): it warns once naming the cover, counts it in Kit.TAPS (Dev panel "taps"), and fires the button when the cover is invisible. A refused press (pending) bounces the button and shows its reason.
- 2026-11-20 (fix-respawn, nate: "unresponsive buttons when trying to talk to npcs or start side quests"): every giver press answers within a frame: a "..." line cut in from the giver (2.5 s), the server's line cuts in over it, a refusal names its reason (loading, too far, level, gone, not active, done), and with no answer in 2.5 s a toast says to try again; side quest prompts hold 0 s; sqStart and sqHold toasts are urgent (shown in every popup mode); the Journal's act buttons show pending.
- 2026-11-20 (prologue-cine, nate 2026-11-19 21:01Z: "dont have the player run to the gold marker. whole prologue needs to be cutscene with qtes until subway"): every beat from the ceremony to the subway fight is a directed cinematic. Shared.PrologueCine authors each beat as segments (a run along the street or the tunnel, an arc: vault, roll, hop, the slow-mo leap, a ledge climb, the drop; or a stand: the Spark, the suit), each with its HeroAnim clip, start and landing one-shots, a camera shot (track, front, side, low, wide, top, over, orbit; cut or eased) and optionally the prompt that starts it, the moment PrologueFx draws for it and what a miss looks like. Fx.PrologueQte plays it: the controls rest from the ceremony to the fight (InputController.freeze), the body moves only on the authored paths (MoveController.travel, new `upright` flag, through everything like a fast-travel flight) with HeroAnimator.cineMode posing it, and the camera is CinemaClient's follow. No gold markers (StoryHud skips the directed beats; Config.Ui.prologue.goals is empty), no HUD verbs before the fight. At the fight: FIGHT!, control, camera and HUD come back.
- 2026-11-20 (prologue-cine): the prompts: run off the plaza DODGE (a fleeing man) and JUMP (a knocked-over barrier); the Spark is a SHIELD (Vigil) or SMASH (Syndicate) prompt on the attack key that fires OpeningAct 1; the leap one JUMP into the slow-mo arc; the chase JUMP per hop, DODGE on roofs 4 and 6, and roof 5's hop falls short: GRAB (Catch: B, the pad's X, a tap) the ledge, then climb; the suit a HOLD (Block key, LB, or hold the prompt) whose fill snaps the pieces on; the drop DROP!; the tunnel (decided: nate's "until subway" covers the tunnel run) a chase with DODGE (a roof chunk), JUMP (a debris pile) and a mash BREAK FREE (a beam pins the hero; 6 presses in 3.4 s) before the chamber. A prompt shows 0.6 s before the move in front of it ends; while it waits the hero holds a pose in slow motion (clips at 0.35, the colours dimmed). Misses are near misses (no damage) and retry; the 3rd does it (Config.Story.opening.qte).
- 2026-11-20 (prologue-cine): the server stays the judge: the device drives the real body, so OpeningService's position checks (the spark point, each roof, the subway steps, the chamber line) still move the beats on; the travel is granted each tick on the directed beats (qte.grantRate 140 studs/s, this hero alone in its story); a move waits until the body is within 30 studs of its start (a cut's placing must arrive first, never a flight across the map). The suit is no longer a timed beat: the device sends OpeningAct 7 (schema 1-6 -> 1-7) once the HOLD snapped it, taken after 1 s; stuck timers spark 12 -> 30, chase 90 -> 130, suit 24 (new), spark.auto 6 -> 16 (after the prompt's worst case), the whole prologue's timeout 330 -> 480 (a player who misses every prompt to the help still reaches the fight and the end; tests/cine_test.luau sums it).
- 2026-11-20 (prologue-cine): the run off the plaza follows OpeningPlan.streetPath (straight when the street is open, else round the corner on the grid) because a directed move passes through buildings; route gains runPath and roofHalf (the ledge). PrologueFx.slowLeap only looks now (the runner moves the hero); the Leaped hook is gone. Storyboard: art/prologue/storyboard.luau + art/blender/storyboard.py + art/prologue/storyboard_sheet.py render the game's own camera for every segment (art/preview/prologue_street.png, prologue_roofs.png, prologue_tunnel.png).

- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, nate: "weapons/powers aren't really causing any sort of destruction. the mobility is"): root cause in the runtime path: CombatService blasted a slash, cone or missed shot at its full reach (origin + dir x range), which for a hero against a wall is inside the building, past its 2-stud wall panels, so only floors and pillars were in range or nothing; and basics (200) chipped silently below chunkCost 400. Now BuildingState.wallPoint puts the blast on the first wall along the move within reach + 3 studs (the server's rayDist: the city's parts, then a Damaged building's standing pieces), structure damage grows with the move's damage (Destruction.structPerCoeff basic 480, skill 650 x coefficient, the slot number a floor; a charge adds 0.6 x its level to rows' own structDmg and supers), a chip that breaks nothing yet sends BuildingImpact kind 4 (dust, chips in the wall's colour, a small crack, a soft crunch; 10 a second per server), and a knocked-out piece crunches (wallSmash, louder the more went).
- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, checklist 196 and 198): swing trails. WeaponKit builds an unlit SwingTrail on every melee weapon (along the outer two thirds of the piece reaching farthest from each hand, foot or the shield arm; across the knuckles or boot for fists and the line weapons), and Fx.SwingTrailFx lights it on every device while one of that weapon's move clips plays (8% to 90% of the clip; 160 studs, 10 heroes). Look trail 0 is every melee weapon's own thin slash in its weapon look's colour (so every swing reads); the five named trails (Streak, Ember, Frost, Spark, Shadow) are earned per weapon with Mastery levels 5, 40, 90, 150, 220 (WeaponRules.trailOpen, checked by CreatorService), drawn with the flight trails' uploaded strips. Ranged weapons draw none. Robux trails wait on nate's product ids.

- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, nate: "the first 30 seconds are almost as important as the first 30 minutes"; a cold open before anything else; replace the intro comic with in-engine cutscenes; "make sure it looks very nice and isn't boring"): the intro is now a 45 s in-engine film in the real city round the hero's spot (Shared.IntroFilm pure, Fx.IntroFilm plays it). The server picks the stage (OpeningService.introStage: of the tallest towers within 320 studs and their four street sides, the one whose shots need the least lifting over other buildings and whose cast never stands inside a building) and sends it with OpeningIntro; StoryClient plays it through CinemaClient as a live camera (letterbox, SKIP after 1.5 s, the creator after), else the comic page (Config.Story.opening.film.on = false, or no stage). Cold open 0-13 s: Lodestar streaks down the street chased by three Crane Troopers firing, smashes into the tower's face (KRA-KOOM), bursts out of its roof (SKRASSH), a drone blows up (BLAM), the landing cracks the street (THOOM) and CAPELINE slams in over the boss track. Then 15 shots of 2-4 s: a runner hunted, the drone's scan (SPARK DETECTED), Silas Crane on the roof, the three fakes landing, Radiant's face cracking to metal (KRZZT), Lodestar for the Vigil, a masked Syndicate crook, the drones rising between the roofs, down to you in the crowd and the spark (ZAP), white flash into the creator. The tower really breaks on the device (BuildingFx.predict held for the film, then heals; the server's city never changes). Order unchanged: intro, creator, prologue; no quick start.

- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, Everything Left 273): weather. Shared.WeatherPlan picks one weather a 10 min slot from a seeded roll of server time (clear 6, overcast 3, rain 3, storm 1, fog bank 2 of 15; nothing but clear twice in a row, a storm only after rain), so every player on every server sees the same sky with no remote; the new weather fades in over 25 s. Fx.WeatherFx draws it: the Atmosphere and Clouds ease to the kind's look (clear keeps the place's own sky), a separate WeatherGrade ColorCorrection (the place's Grade untouched), rain in a box over the camera led by its speed (drops 140/300/520 a second low/medium/high, splash rings on the ground under the camera on medium and high; textures rain.png 87074348735648 and splash.png 120260533473380 from art/fx/textures.py --rain, approved), and storms flash (two pulses, a far neon bolt) with thunder 1.2-3.5 s later. No weather underwater (DeepClient keeps the Atmosphere), in the HQ, a mission pocket or the opening's held sky. A look only: no stat, hit or pay changes. Thunder borrows the collapse rumble pitched down, and the rain has no sound bed, until recorded clips land (NEEDS NATE). Studio: Workspace attribute WeatherForce holds a kind.

- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, Everything Left 480): client errors reach analytics: Telemetry counts every ScriptContext.Error on the device and sends kind 6 at most once a minute (the count and the first errored script's name as AnalyticsSchema.nameHash, a number from 1 to 999; no text on the wire); the server maps the hash back from its own copy of the client scripts and logs client_error (module, device), and on leaving session_errors (device, clean yes/no; value the session's client errors) for a crash-free session rate. Both are in NOSAVE (device facts). tests/uisim.luau learns the Error event (one word).

- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all, nate 2026-11-19: "destroy EVERYTHING, including silos and terrain, and fly through buildings"): audit of what still could not break or be knocked around, after fix-destroy and list-combat (Tasks "Studio list: destroy everything"): street props, trees, silos, farm and outskirts pieces, Old Town and every hub body: already wreckable or knocked loose (tests: Destroy audit, 0 left). NOT before this lane: (1) the park's structures (bandshell, carousel, boathouse, Bow and stone bridges, rink, terrace and small fountains, gate pillars and signs): Blender looks over hidden collision boxes, never broke; (2) the Deep's coral heads and small wreck props once Art.Deep is in (their boxes are hidden, the street break skipped hidden parts); (3) vehicles under rams, heavy landings and boss slams (only power and weapon hits flipped them; a ram stopped dead on a car); (4) park Terrain craters filled with water below the sea line (-1, the lawns sit at 0); (5) a heavy hit on the open sea did nothing (no splash). Kept whole on purpose: the Docks crane (a beacon stands on it), the Crater's lab ruins (PvP cover, already ruins), HQ rooms and props (safe areas never break), mission and side quest pocket props (safe areas; mission geometry; their break targets already break through SiteKit), the Sunken Line's train cars and the sunken factory (rescue and fight geometry), caches (pickups), the park's reeds and lily pads (no collision box; look only).

- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, SPEC CHANGE by nate, approved 17:26Z): the mobility upgrade rework. Each pick's ranks 1, 2 and 4 stay flat number bumps (the existing rows; rows 3 and 5 keep their number bumps too, so every cap test holds), and ranks 3 and 5 each also open a CHOICE of two new moves (Config.Move.choices and each row's `choice`; the 20 moves and their numbers in Config.Move.moves). The hero picks one per rank once trained (free, anywhere, the first time) and swaps free at a safehouse or HQ (SafehouseService.canSwap, so the existing Quick Swap pass rule applies as for loadouts); saved per hero per pick as character mobMoves (save v16, 10 numbers: Shared.MoveRanks.slotOf), server-checked by MoveChoose (AbilityService, HeroService.chooseMove; HeroService.hasMove gates every move's server path). The LOADOUT MOBILITY tab shows each choice rank's two moves as cards with their icons (a third icon set, sheet icons_8 115546856197191; rank 5's gold-ringed) and text. Earned only, never sold; no move changes a stat; knocks and stuns on heroes follow PvpRules compression. Flight: Sonic Boom Dive / Slipstream (r3), Phase / Blazing Ram (r5; nate's sheet said Comet, an old-lore name the text checks refuse: NEEDS NATE if he wants another name); Super-speed: Tornado Run / Afterimage, Sonic Wake / Blink; Leaping: Impact Landing / Wall Crusher, Meteor Strike / Moon Jump; Acrobat: Kick Chain / Ceiling Drop, Shadow Flip / Slingshot; Swing: Double Zip / Yank, Wrecking Kick / Sky Hook.
- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, mobility moves): the 20 moves are in. Shared.MoveActs holds their maths and wire numbers, Services.MoveActService checks each on the server (hasMove, its own cooldown with 15% leeway, MoveGuard history, range) and hits through the new CombatService.moveHit (statuses and knocks to flagged heroes go through pvpHit, so PvpRules compression and the PvP knock cap apply), breaks through BuildingService, VehicleService, CraterService and ImpactService (ramHook, biteHook, swingWall), Controllers.MoveActClient sets off and draws them (FxLib, GadgetFx.decoy, existing juice sounds), MoveController carries the moves that change movement. Speeds: every move sits inside its pick's cap except Blink, Slingshot, Ceiling Drop and Double Zip, which are server-granted distances like Dash Strike; Slipstream's wake adds a 'slip' buff that MoveMath.guardCap and the ally's cruise both read; Phase uses the fast-travel collide-nothing group and holds the hero above the street. Yank lets the Swing hold what it pulls in even when its class could not lift it (an earned move). New tuning numbers added beside nate's moves: phase.clear 3, slipstream.hold 0.6, impactLanding craterDmg 1500 and cooldown 2, wallCrusher cooldown 1, yank cone 18 and stun 1.5, wreckingKick cooldown 1.5. Sonic Wake and Tornado Run read the server's own speeds; Moon Jump, Kick Chain and Sky Hook are device-only feel inside the caps.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 2, coordinator: robots should stop hitting a broken park look's invisible boxes): the server keeps the park's break state too, cheaply: ParkBuilder.groups (one per look, its boxes and bounds from StreetBreak.parkDress), ParkBuilder.breakAt from every BuildingService.impact (a bounds early-out, then at most 4 looks a knock; a big look only from a heavy hit, or a ram the server backs with MoveGuard's peak speed) turns the boxes' CanCollide and CanQuery off and sets DressDown; CityBuilder.heightAt answers the park's Terrain ground inside a broken look's bounds, so robots walk through where it stood. After the street's 45 s, once no hero stands inside, the boxes come back (ParkBuilder.step, once a second). Devices index the Park and Deep folders as they stream in: a box going DressDown breaks the look there too (quietly past 260 studs), and the look stands when the server lets go; a device's own break of a look the server did not take down stands on its own timer as before.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 2, heavier collapses): a collapse or a falling storey's dust rolls out along the ground: from each side of the building a low wide front of the smoke sheet runs out (1.6 x the building's width, 40 to 130 studs) until it meets the next wall (one ray each, once), so it pours down the streets (Presets collapse.roll fronts: 4 / 6 / 8, inside the dust slot's cap); the camera shake falls off with distance ((1 - d / range)^2, a taller building shakes harder and wider; was a flat shake inside 250 studs); when a fall lands, a ground dust ring and a thud shake, and the pieces farthest out tumble off loose onto the street (collapse.spill 4 / 8 / 14 within the debris budget); thrown debris is heavy, grippy and a touch springy (density 2.4, friction 0.8, elasticity 0.32) so it bounces a little and settles.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 2, weak spots found): (1) windows never shattered: a knocked-out wall panel now sprays pale glass shards (the effects library's shard sheet, collapse.glass 4 / 8 / 12 a pane, at most 3 panes a batch) with the uploaded shatter sound; (2) street lamps and traffic lights only ever flew off whole: at the outer 55% of a knock's reach they bend over away from it about their foot (24 to 42 degrees, a 0.18 s Back spring), their lamp goes out, and they are built straight again after 45 s; nearer, they fly as before. Already there and kept: hydrants spray, vending machines spill, bins throw paper (CityLife), billboards and news screens fall with their wall (fix-destroy), roof units and water towers fling and burst (pt-city).
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 3, bosses wreck the city): every boss strike also wrecks along its own shape (ImpactRules.bossWreck, Config.Destruction.bossWreck): a charge or beam every 18 studs down each line (its width + 2 studs round), a cone at a third and two thirds of its reach, rock falls and shots where they land, a stomp in its middle, a swat at roof height; at most 6 spots a strike, each BuildingService.hit 1,200 (3 pieces, 6 to 22 studs, kind 2: pins, collapse cap, street knock and danger as any hit; below the crater threshold, so no crater trail) and VehicleService.hit (district cap). BossSim's strike events now carry lines, cones and columns' geometry. As it walks the boss's feet knock the street round them (BuildingService.street heavy, bodyR + 4 studs, every 0.45 s it moved) and flip the cars there.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 3, terrain digs deeper): a heavy hit inside a live crater (0.8 of its radius) digs that crater one level deeper (up to 3 levels, each 0.6 x the depth more) and starts its life again instead of a new crater beside it; it counts toward the 3-a-second cap. Terrain is read once over the deepest bowl and re-carved from that read; while a crater heals its bowl fills back in 4 steps (4 voxel writes, not every frame). Devices draw a deeper part crater wider and darker with a taller rim. A crater 7+ studs across throws up clods of the ground's own colour and material (Terrain's material colour on the lawns and seabed; Art.Rubble, unanchored, bouncy) within 260 studs of the camera: Presets collapse.clods 3 / 5 / 8 inside the debris budget, faded after 5 s.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 3, fly-through): checked headless on the real city (the 30 thinnest block buildings and 30 across the city, at cruise 60 and Boost 110): 123 of 124 runs go straight through in at most 4 rams (the one left is a 132-stud Uptown tower at cruise whose wall holds; Boost goes through it). The stall was on the device: a second ram on the same building inside 0.25 s (sameWall) was skipped, so the far wall of a block up to about 12 studs deep at cruise and 22 at Boost stopped the hero dead (33 far walls in those runs). MoveController now skips it only when the contact is within RAM.radius (10) of the last hole, a wall that held (a small hook in ramStep and groundRam). The holes open on both faces and the pieces fly out slower than the hero, so debris trails behind.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 4, worst-case load): a headless worst case holds the destruction budgets (Config.Destruction.load, test "worst case load"): 30 heroes wrecking Downtown (a super every 2 s, a skill every 0.5 s, rams flat out inside the impact caps), a boss beam every 2 s and 3 collapses at once. Measured: 0.09 ms a frame of building work (budget 1), the 2 Hz loop's frame 0.2 ms (budget 4), the join snapshot 3.3 KB with 70 buildings Damaged (budget 48 KB). Over budget and fixed: BuildingDiff was 15.5 KB a player in the worst second (the removed pieces as a table of three numbers each); the pieces now go as a 5-byte-a-piece buffer (BuildingState.packRemoved, removedList reads both forms): 3.2 KB (budget 8 KB). A Low device with 3 collapses in view drew 180 big dust particles a second on top of the effects library's 220: collapse dust and its rolling fronts now scale by Presets collapse.dust (Low 0.5, Medium 0.8, High 1): 90 a second on Low (budget 110, half its effects budget).
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 4, bodies into walls): a robot knocked or launched into a wall, a robot swung into one and a hero thrown through one now send BuildingImpact kind 6 with the flight's direction: the wall's own chips and dust burst out of the far side (FxLib smash moment), a puff back out of the entry hole, cracks round it, the clash crunch and a shake that falls off with distance; the holes, glass and bits come with the diff right behind. A wall that holds (never breaks, or past the caps) takes a dent (kind 4 chips and a crack mark, BuildingService.dent). A robot a knockback breaks through a wall flies on through the hole on a launch arc (Combat.impact.robot.through: 12 studs, 3 up, 45 studs/s), tumbling as launched robots do, instead of stopping dead in it.
- 2026-11-20 (destroy-all round 4, throws): pick up and throw was already complete (ThrowService, ThrowClient, ThrowFx); the weak spot was the hit: a throw into a wall just dropped at its foot and only a heavy one did structure damage on the ground. Now a throw that meets a wall in flight smashes it there when it arrives (ImpactService.throwWall: Config.Throw.weights wall 400 / 1,000 / 2,400 within 6 / 8 / 10 studs, the kind 6 moment, inside the impact caps; else a dent), and the robots a throw hits are thrown by its weight (knockKind: light strike as before, medium heavy, heavy charged), so they tumble off and through the walls they meet. Throw damage to robots is unchanged.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass, audit of all 6 bosses on the real paths): already strong and kept: the BREAKING NEWS flash and the 3 s name card (all 6 have titles and portraits), hazard-tape telegraphs for every attack shape (dashes, chevrons, fill motion: not colour alone; steady with Reduce flashing), the plates breaking off at each phase, the slow-mo finisher on each phase and the kill, the result card with the MVPs, a counter for every mobility pick (boss_counter_test), and no quiet stretch (a headless 5-hero fight never goes more than 3.6 s without a warning or a strike on any boss). Weak and fixed, below: hits on the boss barely showed between phases; a phase was a banner without weight in the world; a new hero far below the boss's level spent up to 38% of the fight Downed.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass, hits read on the boss): every Boss.State showing hp lost flashes the boss pale for 0.14 s (at most every 0.45 s; none with Reduce flashing), and every 4% of its max hp lost knocks 2 / 4 / 6 chunks (Low / Medium / High) of its own debris colour off a random piece of its body with a burst and a clank: they bounce on the street and fade after 5 s, inside the debris budget (Config.Fx.boss). Draw only: hit boxes never change.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass, phases): a phase change also sends a shockwave in the phase colour out over the ground from the boss's feet (up to 120 studs), a dust ring and a heavy shake for cameras within 500 studs, on top of the finisher, the banner and the plates.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass, a new hero's first boss; NEEDS NATE to OK): a hero 8 or more levels below the boss takes at most 30% of its max health from one boss hit (Config.Boss.newHero, BossMath.newHeroHit, before its mitigation). Measured beside four level 35s: a level 6 hero was Downed up to 38% of a fight (one hit took 61% of its health); now 13% at most, and it always earns a tier (Participant) through the 25% floor rule as before. Not a spec number (the boss's damage formula is unchanged for everyone else); the alternative nate could pick instead is sidekicking at world bosses (spec H4 says missions only).
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass 2, MVP spotlight): when a boss falls the server names its top 3 (B.contribution.mvp) to everyone (BossAttack k "mvp"); 3 s later, after the collapse, every client within 700 studs lights each MVP where they stand for 8 s: a gold light column welded over them, a gold outline, an "MVP #n" tag with the name, a burst and a ring at their feet, and a "MVP: names" banner (Config.Fx.boss.mvp). Draw only.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass 2, the reward moment): a win now rains the loot onto each fighter 2.5 s after the kill: Credit coins (one per 150 Credits, 6 to 16) and one orb per gear drop (up to 5) arc out of the boss onto the hero, ticking up in pitch as they land, a ring at the feet on the last (JuiceFx.bossLoot, Config.Fx.boss.loot); the result card follows once it lands, its rows pop in one after another (the tier row overshoots like a stamp; an MVP's reads gold "MVP!"). Reduce flashing: no spin, rows appear at once. The pay itself is unchanged.
- 2026-11-20 (boss fights pass 2, finding the first boss): audit: the boss marker hid past 5,000 studs and showed no distance; a hero who cannot fly (most new heroes at the first boss) tapping the MAP's red diamond only got "Unlock flight to fly there"; the alerts said nothing on how to get there. Now the marker shows from anywhere with "NAME 820m"; that MAP tap fast travels to the found beacon nearest the boss when it saves 600+ studs (Travel.nearestFound, the usual MoveFastTravel checks), else says how far and to follow the red marker (and, with no beacon found yet, that beacons unlock fast travel); the RISES IN N MINUTES toast and the boss BREAKING NEWS flash add "Open the MAP and tap the red diamond to get there". Fliers still fly there on the tap.

They wear Art.Alive (art/blender/alive.py, uploaded 116168799962824, 42 meshes, 18 baked texture sets: worn paint and rust, brick courses and soot, panel seams and bolts) through LifeKit like rounds 1 and 2, but visual only: every box stays as it was (collisions, queries, the terminals' prompts, landing on crane tops) and the terminals', canopy's, cranes' and chimneys' boxes are hidden under their meshes (Config.Life.alive.hide). - 2026-11-20 (alive-world, "traffic reacting to fights"): a car within 55 studs of a drawn robot or a big hit, or with one on the road 35 ahead (LifePlan.feared), stops, honks, waits 0.7 s, U-turns across the road on a half loop (LifePlan.uturn, 1.8 s) and races off at 1.45 x cruise for 8 s, hazards blinking on medium and high only (Config.Life.traffic.fear). The device's own robots (EnemyRenderer.each) are the fights, so nothing new goes over the network.
- 2026-11-20 (alive-world): harbour gulls (pigeon meshes x1.7 in gull white) circle over each Docks crane near the camera (1 colony of 4 on phones, 2 of 6 medium, 3 of 9 high) and wheel out and up from a hero or a big hit for 7 s; the Foundry's chimneys smoke (1, 3, 3 stacks at 3, 6, 10 particles a second) with a black burst after a big hit. Both found by CollectionService tags the server puts on the boxes (LifeSmoke, LifeGulls), so a place built without Art.Alive still gets them.
- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, mobility move polish, coordinator's follow-up): Phase never leaves a hero in the walls: a flicker that runs out (or a flight that ends) inside a building flies the hero out at 60 studs/s, still a ghost, to the first open spot along the travel line (4-stud steps up to 60), else the nearest open side or straight up (up to 40; Config.Move.moves.phase.exit, MoveActs.phaseExit), and a ghost never lands. Tornado Run is forgiving on touch and gamepad (tornadoRun.easy: 1.1 turns, a 34-stud ring, 42 studs/s, 6 s window, a quarter of samples may dip; every device allows a 10% dip), all inside the server's own check. The movement buttons name the move they set off now (BLINK, METEOR, MOON, 2 LINES). A yanked car flies in on the shared ThrowFly arc before it is held. Each move card says its input in words per device (Config.Move.moves[].how with {Leap} {Fly} {Boost} {Down}, Config.Move.moveKeys: PC keys, pad buttons, touch buttons; MoveActs.howTo).

- 2026-11-20 (alive-world round 3, the missing sounds; coordinator: CC0 from any source, CC BY excluded): kenney.nl, opengameart.org, freesound.org and the GitHub mirrors were blocked from the cloud, so every source is CC0 already on disk from the sounds pass (notes/Tools.md, "The Ambient atlas"). One new atlas, Ambient (11 clips, 83 s, uploaded 87961900044653, approved), built by `art/audio/build.py <root> --only Ambient` so the other five atlases' files, regions and uploads stay untouched (Roblox counts audio uploads a month). The Ceremony's applause is 46 people clapping from the one CC0 clap take (their own rates, pitches and distances) with two CC0 whistles and a synth murmur of voices: built and checked by spectrogram and loudness only, nobody listened to it here, so a real cheer recording stays NEEDS NATE. The gasp is the CC0 gasp as 14 voices; the scanner hum is Kenney's low engine under a throb; Patch's radio blip became a walkie-talkie key-up (clip patchRadio); gulls and park birds are our synth; the winch is Kenney's circular engine at a varying speed with ratchet clicks, as long as one trolley run (11.57 s, Config.Life.cranes); the rain bed is two CC0 rain loops over a low drum.
- 2026-11-20 (alive-world round 3): where they play: the crowd's applause loop while it cheers and the scanner's hum while it hovers (2D loops on the device in the prologue; both stop when the prologue ends), the gasp once as the scan locks; gull cries every 7 to 16 s from each colony and as it scatters; the winch as each trolley sets off; the park's own bed inside Config.Park.bounds (AudioController.bedAt: the park is no district); the rain bed under the rain at up to Config.Weather.rain.sound (0.38) x its strength, silent underwater. - 2026-11-20 (list-combat, combat effects polish: weak hit feedback): taking a robot's hit was a red screen edge and a fixed shake. Now the server's HERO_HIT event also carries the robot's spot and a melee bit (appended, so old clients ignore them); sparks spray off the hit hero's chest away from the robot, and your own hero gets a red arrow pointing at the robot, a thud (one per 0.08 s) and a shake; a melee blow or a hit of 12% of your max health is heavy (Config.Fx.hurt). Studio items for this lane are numbered 200-299 from now on.
- 2026-11-20 (list-combat, combat effects polish): a Grappler's claw now bites with weight: on a hero, sparks back along the cable, a snap ring, a heavy clank and a camera jolt for the caught hero, and a taut, thicker cable while it holds; on a wall, sparks, a scorch and a lighter clank (CombatFx.hook onBite; Config.Fx.robots.hook).
